自然语言处理

1. 【2611.14515】AutoScaffold: AI-Guided Scaffolding for Reinforcement Learning of Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14515

作者：Zijian Zhang,Rizhen Hu,Jiangweizhi Peng,Shiyang Li,Sujay Bhatt,Sihan Zeng,Mingyi Hong

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：outcome rewards reduce, produces rich training, Reinforcement learning, rich training experience, verifiable rewards

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) produces rich training experience, yet outcome rewards reduce each attempt to a single number and leave most of this information unused. Training-time scaffolds, such as strategies or hints added to the prompt during training, offer a natural way to turn this information into guidance, but their design and adaptation are largely left to humans and tailored to specific domains. We introduce AutoScaffold, a unified AI-for-AI framework that turns a student's training experience into adaptive scaffolds, with minimal domain-specific information and no human intervention during training. A frozen teacher periodically examines this experience, diagnoses bottlenecks, and designs scaffolds in two forms: (i) strategies shared across a class of tasks and (ii) hints for individual hard tasks, adjusting the content and amount of support as the student learns. The scaffolds are removed at evaluation. We apply the same framework to ALFWorld, search-based QA, mathematical reasoning, and GPU kernel generation. When evaluated without scaffolds, students trained with AutoScaffold outperform those trained with the corresponding RL baselines in all four domains, with gains of 7.2 points in ALFWorld success and 4.0 points in average QA exact match. On GPU kernel generation, AutoScaffold increases the fraction of tasks with a correct kernel that runs faster than the PyTorch reference by up to 5.0 points. On math, our student surpasses QuestA's reported accuracy using approximately one-third as many student training steps. Analyses show that the teacher identifies a different failure in each task class and shifts its scaffolds from step-by-step workflows to targeted fixes as the student improves, and that its revised scaffolds help the student learn more than keeping the previous scaffold or using no scaffold.

2. 【2611.14514】Contrastive Discovery: Open-Ended Scientific Discovery over Competing Explanations

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14514

作者：Ziang Liu,James J. Kim,Yijia Dai,Jennifer J. Sun

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：evaluate hypotheses pointwise, LLM agents, data-driven scientific discovery, testing an empirical, empirical claim

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 55 pages. Project page: [this https URL](https://codiscovery.ai/contrastive-discovery/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:LLM agents are increasingly used for open-ended, data-driven scientific discovery, yet most of them evaluate hypotheses pointwise, testing an empirical claim $h$ only against its complement $\neg h$. When evidence rejects $h$, the agent learns that one explanation is unlikely but not which alternative the evidence supports. To recover that alternative, we introduce Contrastive Discovery, which replaces the single hypothesis with a contrast set, a scientific question paired with mutually exclusive and collectively exhaustive competing explanations. Each experiment is scored by how its evidence redistributes belief across the set, and the question is resolved when belief concentrates on one explanation. Because contrast sets change only the unit an agent proposes and scores, an existing agent can use them to re-evaluate evidence it has already collected or to guide its search. Across six domains, $89\%$ of the pointwise candidates with the largest belief shifts are rejections. On the same evidence, contrast sets resolve $73\%$ of these candidates to a specific alternative. When contrast sets guide search, the agent resolves $67\%$ more questions than pointwise surprisal under the same experiment budget. In a blinded study, $38$ human experts chose the same explanation on $78\%$ of resolved questions and on $82\%$ of questions where the pointwise agent rejected its hypothesis and stopped. Together, these results support explicit competing explanations as a way for discovery agents to resolve more scientific questions from the same evidence and validation budget.

3. 【2611.14493】Comparative Analysis of Agent Evaluation Frameworks: Stability, Detection, and Discovery in Enterprise Analytics Agents

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14493

作者：Howard Zhang,Chuck Arvin,Logan Brown,Mihir Bhatia,Neelu Choudhary,Lily Miao

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Software Engineering (cs.SE)

关键词：Deploying LLM-based analytics, enterprise settings requires, reliably detect failures, multi-tool workflows, analytics agents

备注： Accepted to KDD 2026, Jeju, Korea. 9 pages, 3 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Deploying LLM-based analytics agents in enterprise settings requires evaluation frameworks that can reliably detect failures across complex, multi-tool workflows. We present a three-phase comparative study of three evaluation frameworks, each representing a distinct evaluation paradigm (trace-based LLM judging, text-only LLM judging with red-teaming, and deterministic heuristics), applied to two analytics agents in a controlled research setting using frozen execution traces. Phase 1 quantifies evaluation harness stability through repeated measurements, finding that 97% of configurations meet the stability threshold and that stability patterns are agent-independent. Phase 2 measures known risk detection on 72 ground-truth test cases, where the trace-based framework achieves the best precision-recall balance (F1=0.90) while text-only evaluation matches on recall but generates 5x more false alarms. Phase 3 evaluates unknown risk discovery, uncovering 24 novel failures with zero overlap between functional testing and security red-teaming. Our results demonstrate that (1) the information available to the evaluation harness determines evaluation quality, (2) functional and security evaluation are complementary, and (3) LLM-based evaluation harnesses surface subtle errors that human reviewers miss. We recommend a two-framework strategy: trace-based evaluation for functional assessment and adversarial red-teaming for security validation.

4. 【2611.14483】Mechanistic Interpretability Needs a Logic of Inference

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14483

作者：Zhimin Hu,Chen Jiang,Hanbo Xie,N.Apurva Ratan Murty,Sashank Varma

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：networks are increasingly, increasingly studied, internal computations, Neural networks, scientific inquiry

备注： NeurIPS 2026 Interp4Discovery (Oral)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Neural networks are increasingly studied not merely as predictive systems, but as complex systems whose internal computations are themselves objects of scientific inquiry. Recent progress in mechanistic interpretability (MI) has made it possible to identify neurons, features, circuits, population structures, and dynamical patterns associated with model behavior. As these tools improve, attention is shifting from what can be observed inside a model to how those observations support mechanistic explanations. We examine the parallel efforts of neuroscience and MI to address this question and identify recurring inferential bottlenecks when inferring mechanisms from complex neural systems. We propose five principles and organize them into a recursive logic for a more rigorous procedure of mechanistic inference. These principles provide a framework that supports scientific discovery in two senses: it helps turn local interpretability findings into cumulative knowledge of mechanisms within models, and it makes those mechanisms sufficiently precise to generate testable hypotheses about domains beyond the models. Evidence beyond MI is ultimately required to establish what model mechanisms reveal about the world.

5. 【2611.14480】Pterodactyl: Pushing the Limits of Efficient Automated Translation Evaluation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14480

作者：Evelyn Y. Garland,Carola F. Berger

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：automated quality evaluation, Group submission, shared task, task on automated, quality evaluation

备注： 6 pages, 4 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In this paper, we present 1020 Group's submission to the WMT26 shared task on automated quality evaluation. Our system, Pterodactyl, was engineered specifically for subtasks 2 and 3, that is, segment-level quality score prediction and the detection of error-free segments. Pterodactyl is a highly customizable, ultra-low cost system optimized for binary classification in translation triage and data curation. It is based on an LLM-as-a-judge approach, with prompt engineering and model selection guided by ROC analysis. We also developed several variants of Pterodactyl that were specifically adapted for subtasks 3.

6. 【2611.14477】FERN: Cross-Benchmark Table Retrieval for Text-to-SQL with Frozen Retrievers and Learned Ranking

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14477

作者：Arjun Rao,Nishant Gopinath

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：large pooled catalogs, BIRD, systems over large, Spider, RASL

备注： 19 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Text-to-SQL systems over large pooled catalogs must first retrieve the tables a question needs. We compare against the published table recall of RASL, which uses Claude 3.5 models to extract keywords and select tables, on pooled BIRD, Spider, and Fiben catalogs. FERN (Feature-Engineered Ranking with No generative LLM) instead fuses three frozen retrievers into 100 candidates, describes each with up to 120 features, and reorders them with LightGBM rankers fitted once on BIRD's training questions. On Spider, where it was not fitted, its candidate pool alone reaches RASL's published recall at fifteen tables (98.0), and the ranker raises the share of questions with every gold table in the top five (complete@5) from 83.9% to 95.6%. At five tables, FERN's recall is within one point of RASL's on Spider (96.7 vs 97.0) and on BIRD with its evidence hints (96.6 vs 97.5), and 8.2 points below on BIRD without them (89.3). On 121 real BEAVER questions, without BEAVER fitting, its recall at fifteen (82.1) falls between two BEAVER-trained retrievers (80.8, 84.5), but its complete@15 is below both (49.6 vs 54.5 and 62.0). On Fiben, it falls far short at fifteen tables (54.2 vs 69.2, different question sets), although its recall at fifty is numerically close to RASL's question-only retrieval result (80.7 vs 79.4). On BIRD dev with evidence, non-retrieval features raise complete@5 by 7.7 points over retrieval signals alone. On BIRD dev, median latency is 208 ms per question on one A100, without hosted model calls.

7. 【2611.14459】When does text encoding matter for language modeling?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14459

作者：Eugene Jang,Catherine Arnett,Terra Blevins

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：introduces cross-lingual inequities, cross-lingual inequities due, models introduces cross-lingual, facto encoding standard, language models introduces

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:UTF-8's role as the de facto encoding standard in language models introduces cross-lingual inequities due to its variable-length design. However, the effect that this encoding choice has on model performance remains largely unexamined. In this work, we investigate how text encoding impacts the performance of byte-level language models, comparing UTF-8 with a wide range of other encoding strategies, including other Unicode-based approaches, language-specific encodings, and our own customized encodings. To facilitate our analysis, we propose Aether: a hybrid encoding scheme that uses script-specific encodings for our target setting while maintaining full Unicode coverage through UTF-8 fallbacks, using this framework to model four orthographically diverse languages. Surprisingly, we find that while languages with information-dense characters (Chinese, Korean) significantly benefit from compressive encodings, languages whose characters carry less information individually (Georgian, Tamil) are largely insensitive to encoding choice. In light of these findings, we argue that text encoding should be a language-specific design decision, rather than a universal default.

8. 【2611.14421】Earlybird: Predictive Streaming Speech Recognition in 29M Parameters

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14421

作者：Sahil Mahendrakar

类目：ound (cs.SD); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Audio and Speech Processing (eess.AS)

关键词：typically 90-400, speech recognizers output, recognizers output, Streaming speech recognizers, word

备注： 9 pages, 3 figures, 6 tables. Model: [this https URL](https://huggingface.co/sahilmahendrakar/Earlybird-29M)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Streaming speech recognizers output a word only after the speaker has finished it, typically 90-400 ms later. For a voice agent every millisecond counts: the sooner the words are outputted, the sooner the agent can start a search or plan its reply. We present Earlybird, a 29M-parameter streaming recognizer (16.8 MB at 4 bits). Earlybird learns from audio cut off at random points, including partway through a word, and is asked to predict every word that is due by then in full, plus a guess of the next two words. Its raw token probabilities give a confidence for every word including the one still being spoken. On clean audiobook speech (LibriSpeech test-clean), the median word is predicted correctly 90 ms before the speaker finishes it, while released streaming models get it 90 to 400 ms after it ends. Earlybird is the earliest on all six test sets we time, covering audiobooks, TED talks, public speech, earnings calls, and synthetic speech. On the same audiobooks, 69% of words are predicted correctly before they end, against 3-25% for the other models. For words still being spoken, confidence is calibrated, with an expected calibration error (ECE) of 3.7 to 5.5. On the eight English Open ASR Leaderboard sets, it is more accurate than Moonshine Tiny on every set and roughly ties Whistle, a model of the same size.

9. 【2611.14413】Bits or permutations? Spending a fixed inference budget on weight precision or on option-order averaging in small local language models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14413

作者：Mohammed Nayeem Khan,Logesh L,Aliyas Ahmad Misri

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Local language models, Local language, save memory, memory by storing, bits

备注： 25 pages, 15 figures, 9 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Local language models can save memory by storing weights in fewer bits, and they can reduce sensitivity to the order of answer options by averaging a few reorderings. We ask whether the first makes the second worse, and which is the cheaper way to spend a fixed budget. On Qwen3-0.6B and Qwen3-1.7B with simulated round-to-nearest weight quantization (8, 6, 4 and 3 bits, fp16 reference), read from the logits of the letters A to D on ARC-Easy, ARC-Challenge, MMLU and HellaSwag (300 items each), 6 bits lose no accuracy against fp16 (they score +1.8 (+1.0 to +2.5) and +0.9 (+0.3 to +1.5) points higher, which we do not explain), and 8 bits change it by -0.7 (-1.1 to -0.3) and -0.4 (-0.7 to -0.2) points. At 4 bits the answer-slot skew rises from 0.15 to 0.53 (0.6B) and from 0.07 to 0.12 (1.7B); Gaussian noise at the same weight SNR gives 0.15 and 0.37 (two draws) and 0.19 (one draw), so the control is inconclusive on whether rounding adds slot skew beyond equal-power noise. Flip rates rise but stay below an accuracy-matched baseline of independent uniform errors. Averaging four cyclic orders recovers +12.6 points at 4 bits for 0.6B (+5.3 at fp16). We price bits and passes in one machine-independent unit, weight bytes read times passes. In cost-matched pairs, extra orders beat extra bits only when the weights have 6 or more bits, and the decision table reduces to: use 6-bit weights and spend the rest of the budget on up to four orders, one pass each. Everything is simulated on two small models with the simplest quantizer; a title-level search found no earlier study of quantization together with option order.

10. 【2611.14399】REAL Loss: Optimizing Real Expected Acceptance Length for Speculative Decoding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14399

作者：Yu Tian

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Speculative decoding accelerates, decoding accelerates LLM, accelerates LLM inference, draft model propose, model propose multiple

备注： 19 pages, 10 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by having a draft model propose multiple tokens for the target model to verify in one pass. The speedup is governed by the expected acceptance length (EAL), yet standard training objectives such as cross-entropy, KL divergence, and total variation focus only on distribution matching or single-step overlap. Recent studies instead propose block-level training objectives, aiming to optimize EAL directly. However, these objectives remain surrogates rather than exact formulations of EAL. For example, some average later acceptance over target-distributed continuations rather than the verification branches selected by accepted tokens, introducing a covariance gap that can reverse the gradient direction. We analyze this gap and derive REAL, a real EAL training loss that uses path weights for unbiased estimation and Rao-Blackwellization for variance reduction. In a controlled loss-level comparison with a Qwen3.6-35B-A3B target and a Domino drafter, REAL outperforms WTV, VSD, DBLast, and the static LK baseline on probabilistic serving acceptance length across 9 task subsets and 5 evaluation seeds. These gains require only a change to the training loss, with no modification to the drafter architecture or inference procedure, demonstrating the practicality of real EAL training. A reference implementation of the REAL loss is available at this https URL.

11. 【2611.14387】Blurring the Block Boundaries: Overlapping Block Denoising for Efficient Diffusion Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14387

作者：Amir Mann,Jonathan Fhima,Shaked Brody,Or Avitan,Asif Zamir,Shahar Tsiper,Niv Nayman

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Diffusion Language Models, enabling faster token, Diffusion Language, enabling faster, Language Models

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Diffusion Language Models (DLMs) have emerged as effective alternatives to autoregressive models for latency-critical tasks, enabling faster token generation. Recent advances employ block diffusion with self-editing capabilities, where each block refines tokens iteratively before advancing to the next one. Yet each block is only as fast as its slowest token, and the next block cannot start before it. We suggest Blurring the Block Boundaries (B3), a novel method with which two blocks are refined in parallel within a shared forward pass. B3 calibrates itself on the fly, opening the next block only once the model is ready to start generating it. It is a training-free and hyperparameter-free acceleration method which seamlessly integrates within any self-editing block diffusion model. Evaluated on state-of-the-art DLMs across 10 diverse datasets spanning varied tasks and difficulty levels, B3 results in on par generation quality and 11.5-14.5% speedup compared to the base model, pushing the Pareto frontier for latency-quality trade-offs in DLM inference.

12. 【2611.14364】ARE-bench: Benchmarking Agents' Research Ability in Real-World Researcher Sessions and Workspaces

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14364

作者：Jiahang Lin,Wanying Ren,Wanghan Xu,Huayu Sha,Yang Shu,Zhonghang Lu,Chengjun Pan,Yi Dong,Shichun Liu,Yuyang Hu,Shihan Dou,Honglin Guo,Huijie Lv,Pujun Zheng,Zhe Sun,Yuankai Ying,Yiding Wang,Jiahao Chen,Zhixiong Yang,Dingbo Yuan,Yicheng Zou,Zhecan Wang,Guoxiu He,Xiaoyu Xing,Qi Zhang,Tao Gui,Xuanjing Huang

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：achieve increasingly strong, strong benchmark scores, increasingly strong benchmark, agents achieve increasingly, Research agents achieve

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Research agents achieve increasingly strong benchmark scores, yet these scores do not necessarily translate into reliable delivery in ongoing research projects. Many research-agent benchmarks emphasize standalone tasks and relatively self-contained workflows in clean or curated workspaces. However, researchers ask agents to complete one step within an ongoing project, where requests depend on earlier session decisions and workspaces contain unfinished files, outdated versions, and failed attempts. We introduce ARE-bench to evaluate whether research agents advance ongoing projects while respecting prior decisions and existing workspace state, grounded in real session context and in-progress workspaces. ARE-bench includes 75 tasks built on agent sessions and full workspaces from recruited researchers' own projects, spanning seven research stages, 33 requirements, and 150 sub-requirements, with 1,173 binary checklist items in total. Each task is anchored at a real user request, preserves its requirements, and summarizes the earlier requests still in effect as background. The complete workspace is replayed to that boundary without cleanup, preserving artifacts from earlier turns. Each checklist is compiled from an intent-aligned rubric and credits any valid solution. Across seven frontier models, the strongest passes every checklist item on only 66.2% of tasks, and Design and Execution rank among the hardest stages for every model. Three models pass about 69% of tasks when only delivery contracts and general norms are checked, but just 28.4% to 40.0% once historical and material or workspace requirements are also checked. Evaluating delivery without these contextual requirements can therefore overestimate research agents and mask differences between them.

13. 【2611.14356】EngineAgent: Agentizing On-Device LLM Inference Engines with Inference Stack Hierarchy Awareness and Hardware Abilities

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14356

作者：Zhengxiang Huang,Yujie Sun,Shengheng Chen,Zhaode Wang,Chaoyue Niu,Zeyu Zhao,Chengfei Lv,Fan Wu,Guihai Chen

类目：Operating Systems (cs.OS); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Software Engineering (cs.SE)

关键词：Rapidly evolving LLM, hardware make supporting, Rapidly evolving, evolving LLM architectures, costly system challenge

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Rapidly evolving LLM architectures and heterogeneous mobile hardware make supporting new models in on-device inference engines a recurring and costly system challenge. Each model may require coordinated changes across conversion, graph construction, operators, and hardware backends. Existing coding agents often miss these cross-layer dependencies and struggle to diagnose failures across host--device execution paths. We present EngineAgent, an agentic system for automating on-device LLM model enablement. Its key insight is to separate reusable engine and hardware knowledge from model-specific implementation. Before enablement, EngineAgent constructs a hierarchy-aware engine index that captures engine abstraction layers and cross-layer dependencies, and pre-validates backend-specific build, execution, and debugging workflows using already supported models. For each new model, a builder uses this knowledge to coordinate cross-layer changes, while an auditor checks implementation completeness and drives iterative debugging through device-side validation. Across 12 tasks spanning three mobile LLMs and four engine--backend pairs on CPUs, GPUs, and NPUs, EngineAgent achieves 75% end-to-end success with either of two underlying LLMs, versus 33.3% for the strongest coding-agent baseline. Success requires compilation, on-device execution, and output-logit agreement with reference implementations. EngineAgent also reduces average per-task time and model-API cost relative to baselines using the same underlying LLMs.

14. 【2611.14333】AI Isn't Getting Dumber, We're Getting Pickier: Rising Standards in Human Evaluation of Large Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14333

作者：Zihan Chen,Lei Nico Zheng,Di Zhu

类目：Human-Computer Interaction (cs.HC); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computers and Society (cs.CY)

关键词：Users often complain, models, Users, Chatbot Arena, pairs

备注： 23 pages, 6 figures, 10 tables; working paper

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Users often complain that AI assistants such as ChatGPT are getting worse. Some versions do change, but human evaluation leaves a second possibility untested: the people judging may change. We separate the two on Chatbot Arena, a public platform where people compare answers from two anonymous large language models (LLMs) and pick the better one or say both are bad. We study 1,260,967 blind votes cast from May 2023 to August 2024 on pairs of models whose versions are fixed: dated vendor snapshots and models with fixed open weights. For the same pair of unchanged models, the share of votes saying "both are bad" rises by 1.71 percentage points a month on a mean of 17.1%, and by 1.91 points a month in 2024. In fourteen pairs observed often in both 2023 and 2024, it rose in every one, from 15.5% to 23.3%. The ranking does not trend: in 2024, the stronger model's share of decisive votes is flat. The rise holds within the same voter and on the same pair, and across arrival cohorts from their first recorded vote. It holds with prompt controls, in every major language, for dated vendor snapshots, open-weight models and models with stable answer length, and between interface changes. The drift is shared across models: month effects estimated from other models' pairs remove it from each model's own pairs. Platform-wide, the average model would be expected to score 78% against the 2023 average, yet the share of "both are bad" votes ended near where it began. The pattern fits a standard that follows the category's progress, so a product that stands still is judged as declining. Absolute verdicts on AI drifted while relative comparisons held steady, an evaluation drift that matters for LLM leaderboards and for the human feedback used to train models.

15. 【2611.14325】Point-in-Time Encoders for Earnings Calls

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14325

作者：Alessio Brini

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Statistical Finance (q-fin.ST)

关键词：Text encoders turn, encoders turn earnings, vintage, encoders turn, Text

备注： 10 pages, 3 figures, 7 tables. Accepted at the 3rd IEEE International Workshop on Large Language Models for Finance, IEEE BigData 2026. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/Alessiobrini/point-in-time-encoders-for-earnings-calls)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Text encoders turn earnings calls into vectors for search, for language models that answer from retrieved passages, and for forecasting models. A live retriever can use an encoder trained once on all available text, as finance retrievers are, but a backtest needs an encoder that read no text written after the date of each vector. To address this look-ahead bias, from 412,248 calls we train vintages under four pretraining recipes, with yearly cutoffs from 2010 to 2024, and each vintage reads only text dated on or before its cutoff. We then fine-tune each vintage on the question-answer pairs of the calls themselves. The vintages we train on calls alone, with 33.8M parameters, retrieve answers on held-out calls better than the general retrievers we tested in every test quarter. Their vectors also raise by about half the rank correlation between next-day returns and forecasts built from past returns and firm size. When each vintage continues the previous one, a later vintage retrieves better on earlier quarters than the vintage available at the time, and a point-in-time language model that answers from the passages the later vintage retrieves inherits the gain. The 2024 vintage of our families also forecasts earnings surprises better, although the interval of this gain includes zero on calls that no vintage read. It forecasts returns no better, so point-in-time vintages matter most for retrieval and little for forecasts of returns.

16. 【2611.14309】MMDM: Measuring Massive Multitask Decision Making

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14309

作者：Junhao Shen,Qing Zong,Yue Guo,Zhanqiu Zhang,Yiwen Guo,Hong Cheng

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：single forward pass, directly make structured, make structured probabilistic, structured probabilistic decisions, Models directly make

备注： Project page: [this https URL](https://mmdm-benchmark.github.io/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:System-1 Decision Models directly make structured probabilistic decisions over query-specified options given a state in a single forward pass, without autoregressively generating reasoning trajectories or textual responses. Recent evaluations cover diverse tasks but often rely on existing benchmarks, where prior exposure to test material can obscure generalization. Moreover, real-world decision making often involves long contexts, large option spaces, and complex logical dependencies, challenges that existing benchmarks do not adequately capture together. Thus, we introduce MMDM (Massive Multitask Decision Making), a benchmark of 5,448 high-quality questions across 16 task categories, combining newly constructed tasks with source-grounded recompositions and rewrites for single-select, multi-select, and soft-label decision making in fresh contexts. It covers realistic scenarios such as agent state tracking, tool and skill selection, preference judgments, and rubric scoring, with option spaces ranging from 2 to 1,024 and inputs extending to 64,817 tokens. With MMDM, we conduct a comprehensive quantitative evaluation of 28 decision models, showing that many perform well on common knowledge yet struggle with interdependent options, constrained decisions, and multi-select tasks. For example, Perplexity Decider-V1.2 reaches 78.75% Hard Single-select Accuracy on world knowledge, but only 53.95% on option logic and 47.95% on constraint planning, while the best Hard Multi-select Accuracy across all models is 36.81%. These results highlight joint reasoning, constraint-aware selection, and complete multi-select recovery as key priorities for decision model development, with MMDM providing a testbed for measuring this progress.

17. 【2611.14252】Semantic Chains: From Reachable Facts to Justified Answers

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14252

作者：Misha Andreev

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：connected statements, sequence of connected, fact, full answer, question

备注： 31 pages, 10 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:How can the facts that justify an answer be found from a question and presented as a sequence of connected statements? This paper models a context as a graph of logical facts and takes as given which expressions represent each fact. A parent fact can express a relation among its subfacts, and a preserving edge allows the content of a subfact to be recovered within a full representation of its parent. Suppose a root fact justifies the full answer and a path of preserving edges leads from that root to a fact that designates an object shared by the question and the answer. Under the conditions of the model, a search starting from the objects in the question reaches the root and covers every fact in the selected expansion. The root then admits a text that forms a single link connecting the question to the full answer and justifying it. For chains with several links, the paper gives a complete enumeration of candidate orders within the stated class and specifies when the link texts jointly preserve the justification and support every transition.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14236

作者：Boris Malashenko,Ivan Jarsky,Egor Soldatov,Vladislav Luzhnov,Sviatoslav Stumpf,Vladislav Shmatkov,Viacheslav Shalamov,Andrey Stankevich,Valeria Efimova

备注： Accepted to ReNeuIR @ SIGIR 2026. 16 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Transformer sentence encoders provide strong retrieval quality, but their inference cost becomes a bottleneck when embeddings must be produced on CPUs or refreshed frequently. We introduce StaRSE, a compact Russian static sentence encoder that removes Transformer inference at deployment time: token directions are stored as sign bits, token norms are stored separately, and sentences are encoded by mean pooling over norm-scaled sign directions. Ablations examine adjacent-sentence supervision, token norms, and direct sign-projected post-training.

21. 【2611.14221】Stagewise and interpretable emergence of inside-outside computation in Transformer-based language models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14221

作者：Jack T. Parley

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Disordered Systems and Neural Networks (cond-mat.dis-nn)

关键词：Variability in syntactic, deal with proficiently, fundamental aspects, aspects of natural, natural language

备注： 20 pages, 9 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Variability in syntactic structure and parsing ambiguity are fundamental aspects of natural language which modern LLMs have to learn to deal with proficiently. Previous "post-mortem" studies have shown that fully trained Transformers indeed approximate canonical parsing algorithms such as inside-outside, but little is established theoretically about how this is actually learned. Here, we make a first step towards addressing this by studying the learning dynamics in synthetic context-free grammars with variable parse tree topology. The stagewise learning curves are fully interpretable, in the sense that all of the loss stages a) can be computed analytically, and b) can be clearly associated to the representation of a hidden latent tree variable, which allows to lift ambiguity and utilize information from wider contexts. By probing representations, we visualize the emergence of inside-outside computation across depth and during training: while during early stages local (bigram/trigram) information is used and involves only a few initial layers, gradually the Transformer develops an encoder-decoder structure where the most abstract latent information is encoded in the middle, and the latter half of the network is used to progressively refine the final token prediction. This organization of computation is reminiscent of findings in real-world LLMs, and importantly applies here to a synthetic model which allows full analytical control of the data statistics, thus opening the door to theoretical progress.

22. 【2611.14209】Breakdown of Autoregressive Multi-Step Reasoning Under Concentrated vs. Diffuse Attention Head Dropout

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14209

作者：Karol Struniawski

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：mammalian neocortical networks, silence termed off-states, neocortical networks experience, networks experience localized, experience localized periods

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:During extended wakefulness, mammalian neocortical networks experience localized periods of transient neuronal silence termed off-states. Here, we examine how analogous spatiotemporally concentrated disruptions affect sequential reasoning in autoregressive Large Language Models (LLMs). Evaluating 250 paired derivations on the GSM8K benchmark across two 8B models (Qwen3-8B and LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct), we contrast clustered bursts of attention head masking against budget-matched diffuse i.i.d. dropout. Concentrated bursts induce substantial reasoning degradation: at onset parameter $\mathcal{B}=0.10$ (scheduling $\sim 5.7\%$ of vulnerable head-token volume), Qwen3-8B accuracy drops from $91.2\%$ under diffuse noise to $78.8\%$ under burst dropout ($\Delta = -12.4$ percentage points [pp], exact McNemar $p = 5.5 \times 10^{-6}$), while LLaMA-3.1-8B-Instruct drops from $84.0\%$ to $76.8\%$ ($\Delta = -7.2$ pp, $p = 0.0198$). Conversely, dispersing the identical cumulative budget uniformly across generation steps via stratified quintile matching yields substantially milder degradation (retaining $91.2\%$ in Qwen and $84.0\%$ in LLaMA). In an exploratory factorial dissociation ($N=100$), single-step dropout density is associated with an acute drop in a single schedule draw ($-10.0$ pp on Qwen, $p_{\text{holm}} = 0.0425$), while multi-token continuity exhibits an inconclusive compounding trend ($-7.0$ pp, $p_{\text{holm}} = 0.1892$). Error analysis reveals that Qwen failures reflect derivation errors within formatted responses (conditional accuracy drop of $-11.4$ pp), whereas in LLaMA $22.2\%$ of discordant losses lack a final answer format line (conditional gap narrows to $-5.0$ pp). These findings demonstrate that autoregressive reasoning chains are particularly vulnerable to concentrated structural disruptions, providing empirical benchmarks for fault tolerance in transformer architectures.

23. 【2611.14168】Representational Atoms in Neural Networks

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14168

作者：Chenhui Hu,Pengfei Cao,Kang Liu,Jun Zhao

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Understanding the internal representations of neural networks and characterizing their relationship with human conceptual structure are two critical questions for elucidating model mechanisms. The former reveals how learned knowledge is organized in internal representations, whereas the latter provides a basis for interpreting these representations through human conceptual structure. Here, we formally define fundamental representational units (FRUs) and establish two evaluation criteria: fidelity and stability. We further prove that threshold-activated sparse autoencoders (TSAEs) can identify FRUs with high fidelity and stability under appropriate conditions, and validate this result through large-scale experiments. Further analyses reveal that FRUs with higher stability exhibit greater monosemanticity and can be composed across layers to recover semantic organization corresponding to human conceptual structure. These results provide a unified framework for defining, evaluating and identifying FRUs in neural networks, and establish a foundation for characterizing the relationship between neural network representations and human conceptual structure.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14116

作者：Patricio M. Vera

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV)

关键词：visual similarity explain, objects share, Lewis Frank, self-supervised visual encoder, frozen self-supervised visual

备注： 15 pages, 5 figures. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/patriciomvera/lexical-consensus) . Data: [this https URL](https://huggingface.co/datasets/MrPatoVera/lexical-consensus-data) and [this https URL](https://huggingface.co/datasets/MrPatoVera/lexical-consensus-data-cifar10h)

Abstract:When two objects share a name, how much does their visual similarity explain, and what do the teaching examples add? Second, in an artificial learner using the same encoder, words joining two categories were learned less accurately the farther apart the categories were (84.7%, 65.4%, and 53.0% naming accuracy for near, mid, and far pairs). The secondary human data were analyzed under an internal plan fixed before the confirmatory analyses (not externally preregistered). Visual similarity accounts for part of how generalization is organized in both systems, while the composition of examples contributes what the distance we used does not capture.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14100

作者：Mikhail Kulakov(Novosibirsk State University),Ruslan Solikhov(Novosibirsk State University),Ivan Bondarenko(Novosibirsk State University)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We present our system for the SHROOM-visions 2026 shared task on character-level hallucination span detection in image-conditioned vision-language model (VLM) responses, evaluated across English, French, Italian, and Chinese. The detector is built on a frozen Gemma-4-12B-it backbone with trainable LoRA adapters (approximately 10.7M parameters). Because the backbone is a causal decoder, hidden states read from a single copy of an answer cannot attend to subsequent tokens; we therefore repeat the candidate answer within one prompt and extract features from its second occurrence, giving every position approximate bidirectional context in a single non-autoregressive pass. A character-level locator combines intermediate hidden states to predict hallucination probabilities, BIO boundaries, and a response-level abstention score, while separate language-specific classifiers assign one of five hallucination types to each predicted span. On the closed test set, our system reaches a macro-averaged character IoU of 0.4404, more than doubling the official baseline (0.1854), and ranks 5th-6th on IoU across the four languages. On the English tuning split, controlled ablations attribute a gain to the repeated answer that is comparable to that of LoRA adaptation (its held-out gain is smaller and concentrated on hallucinated answers), and they show that the detector relies on the image-answer pairing: shuffling images across examples drives IoU down to the level of the predict-nothing floor or below it in every language. In deployments where the same Gemma-4-12B-it model also generates answers, its base weights can be reused, making the approach practical for local question-answering systems that cannot run a separate verifier.

26. 【2611.14079】From Bias to Positionality: A critical grammar for Large Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14079

作者：Dave Murray-Rust,Elisa Giaccardi,Federico Bomba,Maria Luce Lupetti

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Human-Computer Interaction (cs.HC)

关键词：machine learning systems, default lens, lens for understanding, understanding what machine, machine learning

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Bias has become a default lens for understanding what machine learning systems do and how we should relate to them. Bias is at root a deviation from a standard; in decision making systems we can supply a standard from outside: a law, a protected attribute, a benchmark, a statistical property. However, even there, biased is often used as a shorthand, without stating what the position taken is - a silent ``relative to''. In a general use of language models, nothing supplies the standard: there is no decision to audit and no counterfactual output, so the shorthand fails. We argue that positionality -- the located, articulated, owned and contestable position from which anything is said -- is the better grammar for the question these models now force: who is speaking, from where? Against the vendors' answer of nowhere and the seductive possibility of everywhere, we argue that when a model is taken as-if speaking, it speaks from somewhere, and this means positionality can describe models, and the way they are believed beyond their warrant, even though they are not accountable speakers. A model's positionality is laminar -- laid down across corpus, tuning, system prompt, application and the live conversation. We separate three sets of factors: the positionality of a model's creators, the behaviour they intend for it, and the behaviour it enacts, and situate current work within this matrix. This lets us highlight what each stratum inherits from the one below, with ruptures and collapses where that inheritance is disavowed. We then return to where models speak from, and discuss the implications for how models are made, read and held accountable.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14036

Abstract:Misogyny is among the most prevalent forms of online hate speech directed at Brazilian users, yet open and auditable resources dedicated to it in Brazilian Portuguese remain scarce. 29. 【2611.14019】Program-Aided Patchable Skill Evolution

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14019

备注： 17 pages, 6 figures, 5 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Agent skills have emerged as an effective means of enabling LLM agents to solve complex, interactive tasks by encoding reusable knowledge without modifying model parameters. Recent approaches further improve skill adaptation by distilling reusable experience from agent execution trajectories and evolving skills based on downstream task feedback. However, existing methods primarily represent skills as natural language documents, intertwining procedural logic with descriptive knowledge and making skills difficult to maintain and evolve. To address this limitation, we propose PASE (Program-Aided Patchable Skill Evolution), a program-aided framework that iteratively evolves skills by associating them with executable programs. Unlike prior approaches that encode trajectory-derived experience primarily in natural language, PASE separates reusable procedural logic into editable programs, enabling targeted updates to executable components while reducing the need to repeatedly revise natural language skills. Extensive experiments across diverse models and benchmarks demonstrate that PASE consistently outperforms all baselines, by achieving over 2.8% improvements. Further analyses show that PASE exhibits greater evolutionary stability and generalisation capability, highlighting the critical role of programmatic knowledge in skill evolution. We will release all data and code via GitHub.

30. 【2611.14010】One Sequence Is Enough: Fingerprinting Language Models from Fixed-Length Integer Sequences

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14010

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Users of model APIs and third-party platforms often rely on the declared model name to identify the model responding to their requests. However, the actual model may differ from the declared one, motivating identity verification based on observable outputs. Previous studies have shown that preferences in simple numerical-choice tasks can serve as model fingerprints. We investigate whether a single fixed-length integer sequence can combine multiple numerical observations to identify its source model. These results show that a single integer sequence contains useful model fingerprints and that additional answers further improve identification.

31. 【2611.14009】Coding Agent Memory Post-Training II: Unlocking the Memory Potential of Pre-trained File Operations for Long-Horizon Tasks via On-Policy Distillation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14009

作者：Lirui Luo,Kelong Mao,Xinwei Yang,Rongqing Li,Luyu Chen,Kieran Wong,Yudong Guo,Xinrui Wang,Jiayin Zhu,Gu Simiu,Sulong Xu,Cong Fang

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Language-model agents increasingly, interaction histories exceed, agents increasingly tackle, Language-model agents, increasingly tackle long-horizon

备注： 40 pages, 6 figures, 8 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Language-model agents increasingly tackle long-horizon tasks whose interaction histories exceed the model's active context. They must therefore decide what to write down and what to read back, and when to do either. Recent work has begun to use on-policy distillation (OPD) to make memory control part of the policy, relying on memory tools and compaction protocols that lie outside either model's pre-training. However, this weakens the distillation on both sides: the teacher cannot give useful guidance on an interface it has never used, and the student samples too few meaningful memory actions for that guidance to take effect. To study this problem, we introduce Coding Agent Memory Gym II (CAMG-II), a suite of long-horizon agentic-RL environments spanning Shop, Coding, AutoResearch, General, and Visual. Alongside each environment's native task interface, CAMG-II provides ordinary Bash access and an episode-persistent workspace, enabling the policy to create, revise, search, and reuse files as memory across context replacements. We also introduce Coding Agent Memory Post-Training II (CAMP-II), which trains a single policy jointly across all five environments with fully asynchronous OPD to learn this file-based memory behavior directly from a frozen teacher with a prior for file operations. On CAMG-II, CAMP-II obtains 2.97 times the gain of the strongest OPD baseline. The memory behavior CAMP-II learns generalizes to two external benchmarks, Terminal-Bench 2.1 and SWE-Bench Pro V2, with improved success rates.

32. 【2611.13997】Specify Privacy Yourself: Assessing Inference-Time Personalized Privacy Preservation Ability of Large Vision-Language Models

作者：Xingqi Wang,Xiaoyuan Yi,Xing Xie,Jia Jia

Abstract:Large Vision-Language Models (LVLMs) have demonstrated remarkable capabilities but raise significant privacy concerns due to their abilities to infer sensitive personal information from images with high precision. 33. 【2611.13990】On the information rate of phones, syllables and words

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13990

作者：Herman Kamper

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Audio and Speech Processing (eess.AS)

关键词：encode speech content, linguistic units minimise, required to encode, speech content, encode speech

备注： 5 pages, 1 table, 2 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Which linguistic units minimise the bitrate required to encode speech content: phones, syllables, words, maybe larger units, or something in between? This question has implications for the development of speech and language systems and theories of human language processing. Using a transcribed corpus of 960 hours of English audiobooks, I compare the information rates of phones, syllables, words, multi-word combinations and intermediate tokenisations. While all units are equivalent under a perfect language model, it makes sense to compare these under limited modelling budgets; I use $n$-gram and transformer language models. The lowest bitrates are achieved using words, with slightly longer units sometimes performing best. The results show that, when modelling capacity is limited, units larger than syllables but smaller than long multi-word phrases provide the most efficient representation in terms of information rate.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13975

作者：Zhenhong Zhou,Mao Zheng,Mingyang Song,Qiankun Li,Kun Wang,Junfeng Fang

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

备注： 55 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) models, the nature of expert specialization remains controversial. Reports of domain-associated expert preferences contrast with analyses attributing routing primarily to token identity or syntactic patterns. To clarify these differing accounts, we look beyond expert-level routing to investigate the neuron-level organization of domain-specialized computation using Routing-Aware Magnitude Profiling (RAMP), a measure developed for this analysis. Using mixed-domain calibration data, we identify domain-specialized neuron sets with 58-74% overlap across multi-source samples of the same domain, compared with 15-24% across domains. To understand how this organization evolves during training, we trace neuron importance throughout pre-training and post-training across three fully open MoE families. Neuron importance continues to reorganize between pre-training checkpoints despite comparatively stable expert-level importance rankings, while post-training largely preserves neuron rankings. Building on these neuron-level perspectives, we further introduce RAMP-based Pruning (RAMPr) to select neurons across experts for balanced multi-domain capability preservation. At 75% neuron retention, RAMPr preserves average 91-98% of original full performance across six models. At 50% retention, our pruning method can reduce resident GPU memory by 36-43% and improves batched prefill throughput by 17-39% effectively. Our code is available at this https URL

点击查看摘要

Abstract:As large language models generate increasingly high-quality responses, recent work on response evaluation has moved beyond response-centered criteria, such as correctness and coherence, toward user-centered criteria, including user preferences and perceived usefulness. This shift is fundamentally important for developing technologies that are truly useful to real-world users. 37. 【2611.13885】Are Causal Neuron Circuits Unique and Transferable?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13885

作者：Ömer Tekin Yavuz(İletişim Yazılım)

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Interpretability demonstrations, Lion King riddle, two-hop Lion King, neurons, Lion King

备注： 12 pages, 5 figures, 4 tables. Code, data and results: [this https URL](https://github.com/omtekyav/causal-neuron-circuits)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Interpretability demonstrations often show that intervening on a group of neurons changes a model's answer. A recent example removes an 18-neuron group in Qwen3-32B and the model answers "Simba" instead of "Scar" to a two-hop Lion King riddle. Such a demonstration establishes that the group matters, but not how much of it is necessary, whether other groups could do the same, or whether the effect carries over to other inputs. We ask: when intervening on a group of neurons changes a model's answer, how much of that group is actually necessary? For every selected group we measure all 4,096 subsets of 12 MLP neurons. On a two-alternative capital task (Qwen3-0.6B), zero-ablating 12 selected neurons lowers the answer margin by 27% while random neurons barely change it, yet replacing the same neurons with their values from other questions also barely changes it: their activations are nearly constant across questions, and we find no evidence that they carry question-specific content. On two-hop relation chains, patching 12 neurons with activations from the first-hop question shifts the answer to the bridge entity (from Scar to Simba): the greedy answer starts with the bridge in 9/11 chains for Qwen3-1.7B, 9/11 for Llama-3.2-1B and 8/10 for Gemma-3-1B; random neurons never do. The full group is rarely necessary: a few neurons often suffice, and in many chains disjoint subsets each flip the answer on their own, in an exploratory analysis most often when the model is less confident. Group effects transfer to rephrasings of the same chain but are much weaker on other tested chains. We study neuron-level causal interventions and alternative subsets; we do not recover the full connectivity of circuits.

38. 【2611.13855】Beyond Passive Imitation: On-Policy Mid-Training Distillation for Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13855

作者：Ruize Zheng,Xuyang Liu,Siyu Ren,Xiaobing Tu,Yinggui Wang,Jinkui Ren,Xiantao Zhang,Linfeng Zhang

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：adapts large language, standard next-token prediction, Continual pre-training, large language models, adapts large

备注： 17 pages, 4 figures, 4 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Continual pre-training (CPT) adapts large language models (LLMs) to specialized domains, yet its standard next-token prediction (NTP) objective treats every corpus token as a fixed target, regardless of its utility to the current model. In a controlled study on the same 1B-token mathematical corpus, we observe sharply different outcomes across base models: NTP improves Llama-3.2-3B's performance on mathematical benchmarks but substantially degrades Qwen3-1.7B-Base's performance, indicating that, under a fixed training recipe, the same training sequences can have model-dependent effects. To address this problem, we introduce On-Policy Mid-Training Distillation (OPMD), which reformulates mid-training from offline text imitation into active, query-driven knowledge acquisition. At selected anchor positions, OPMD samples trajectories from the evolving student policy and applies dense token-level reverse-KL rewards from a frozen teacher, training the model on states it actually visits rather than only on ground-truth prefixes. This on-policy objective reduces the training-generation mismatch of teacher-forced NTP. On MATH-500 and Minerva, OPMD improves the Qwen student over its base performance and outperforms the evaluated NTP baseline, which degrades on both benchmarks. OPMD integrates into CPT pipelines without added inference-time overhead and offers a model-aware alternative to passive domain adaptation.

42. 【2611.13788】LLM Validation Makes Minimal-Pair Benchmarks Easier for Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13788

作者：Guofang Tang

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Quebec French, obvious substitute, substitute for costly, Minimal-pair, Minimal-pair benchmarks

备注： 11 pages, 1 figure

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Minimal-pair benchmarks are only as good as their labels, and LLMs are an obvious substitute for costly human validation. We test it on BLiMP (English) and QFrBLiMP (Quebec French), the two public minimal-pair datasets releasing every forced-choice human judgment. Three LLMs validate by forced choice, by rating, and as a panel, scored against human panels of one, three and five judges in two pre-registered phases. On BLiMP, forced-choice validators agree with held-out human majorities more often than five-judge panels, yet catch far fewer pairs whose label humans reject than one judge does. On both datasets, across 10 open LMs, human-supported pairs that the pre-registered LLM filter flags are 18.9 points harder than those it accepts, in all 70 LM-validator combinations, and harder than pairs a single human judge flags. Filtering thus removes items on which LMs fail but humans do not, raising accuracy more than random removal. A protocol frozen before any output replicates the gap and the lift on Dutch BLiMP-NL. LLM validation makes minimal-pair benchmarks easier for the models they are meant to test; we release code, validator outputs and item-level scores.

46. 【2611.13675】OpticalRAG: Pixel-Space Compression for Token-Efficient Retrieval-Augmented Generation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13675

作者：Senhao Liu,Yuheng Zhang,Chunyu Wei,Yueguo Chen,Xinran Zhang

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：large language models, compression discards fine-grained, discards fine-grained information, Retrieval-augmented generation, one-dimensional token sequence

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is bottlenecked by the token budget of large language models: feeding long documents into the context window is expensive, while aggressive textual compression discards fine-grained information that is irreversibly lost in a one-dimensional token sequence. We argue that this bottleneck is not fundamental to information density but to the choice of compression \emph{modality}. Modern visual encoders can pack the contents of a rendered text page into roughly a hundred visual tokens with little semantic loss, opening a new compression-fidelity tradeoff that purely textual methods cannot reach. We present \textbf{OpticalRAG}, a framework that renders documents as page images, encodes them with a frozen vision encoder, and distills the retrieved visual tokens into as few as $16$ injected tokens before passing them to the LLM. Two ingredients make this work without expensive cross-modal training: (i) \emph{Encoder-Bridged Retrieval}, which projects text-query states into the visual feature space of the frozen encoder, through a projection shared with the compressor, to retrieve visual tokens from a text query, with no contrastive training; and (ii) \emph{Query-Driven Distillation}, a lightweight cross-attention compressor that condenses the retrieved visual tokens into a small set of query-conditioned prefix tokens. On LongBench-v2, BAMBOO, and LooGLE-v2, OpticalRAG achieves the best compression-aware accuracy (CAP) on all three benchmarks while injecting only $16$ tokens, and improves the closed-book Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct backbone by $5.17$ points on this http URL is available at this https URL.

作者：Mannon Ochilov,Oybek Narzullayev,Bahodir Zaripov

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：poor image quality, Uzbek official, share of OCR, technical documents, official and technical

备注： 7 pages, 2 figures, 7 tables. Accepted at the 10th International Conference on Future Networks and Distributed Systems (ICFNDS 2026), ACM ICPS. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/OybekNarzullaev/byt5-uzbek-postocr)

Abstract:In Uzbek official and technical documents, a large share of OCR errors is caused not by poor image quality but by characters that are visually identical across the two alphabets in use. Because the script transition is still under way, Cyrillic and Latin appear on the same page and often within a single sentence, and in our measurements the density of homoglyph errors on such pages is close to five times higher than in single-script text. We study this problem in a setting where the corpus of engineering-geological and ICT infrastructure reports may not leave the organisation, so every stage was executed on local hardware. The proposed system combines a byte-level ByT5 model with a noise generator whose sampling weights are estimated from 359 manually classified error events, together with a Levenshtein-bounded conservative decoding rule. On a Gold set of 5,204 line pairs it lowers the character error rate from 0.0578 to 0.0369, restores 91.3% of the homoglyphs and introduces only 84 new errors.

50. 【2611.13607】One Byte, One Token: Byteifying a 27B Hybrid-Attention LLM with Self-Generated Data

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13607

作者：Liwei Zhang,ZhiMing Tong

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Byte-level BPE fragments, BPE fragments Chinese, fragments Chinese text, Chinese Wikipedia, Byte-level BPE

备注： Full text in English followed by the full text in Chinese. 39 pages, 2 figures, 7 tables. Code and task suite: [this https URL](https://huggingface.co/datasets/leiwhere/ld3-byte-eval)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Byte-level BPE fragments Chinese text (for Qwen3.8 on Chinese Wikipedia, 3.84 bytes per token on average; 64% of tokens are single characters) and hides spelling from the model. We ask whether an already-trained 27B subword model can be converted directly into a pure byte-level model (one byte, one token), without a patching structure or a local encoder, while keeping most of its ability. Starting from Qwen3.8-27B (48 Gated DeltaNet layers + 16 full-attention layers), we shrink the vocabulary from 248,320 to 384 entries (the 256 byte symbols already in the BPE vocabulary plus special ids), leave every other weight untouched, and continue training in a two-stage curriculum (encoding first, then reading) on 4.77 GB of supervised bytes: all dialogue/behavioral supervision is generated on-policy by the original model itself (character dictionary, word dictionary, general dialogue, coding sessions), and knowledge reading uses encyclopedia text (54% of supervised bytes). Training takes 178.6 h on 8xH200. On the same GPU and serving stack, the byte model's gap to the original falls within the paired bootstrap interval on MATH-500, HumanEval+, MMLU-Pro and non-thinking GSM8K, and it loses 4-6 points on C-Eval, thinking-mode GSM8K and non-thinking IFEval. Without thinking it leads on every character-level task (CUTE 75.2 - 84.0, EXECUTE-zho 81.6 - 93.9, our Chinese character suite 54.4 - 64.0), but lookup knowledge about Chinese characters such as pronunciation and stroke count drops, and thinking does not recover it. The cost is throughput: per-step speed is identical, but each step yields 3-5x less output; Chinese characters per second is about 1/4 of the original, and prefill at 64K bytes is 6.5x slower. We also document four byte-model failure modes (greedy runaway, sampling breaking UTF-8, ineffective penalty terms on a small vocabulary, repetition) and their fixes.

51. 【2611.13597】Compute the Interactions, Not the Attributes: What a Fact Layer Must Supply in the Chinese Sexagenary System

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13597

作者：Huixue Han,Jiaying Liu

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Language-model systems, Chinese sexagenary system, rarely measured, rule-governed domains, deterministic fact layer

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Language-model systems in rule-governed domains often pair the model with a deterministic fact layer, but what that layer should compute is rarely measured. We measure it in the Chinese sexagenary system underlying BaZi chart analysis, where every fact has one table-checkable value: 682 items over twelve fact categories and 160 transit--natal collision items, each posed on a ladder of rungs that differ only in how much of the computation is done for the model. Answering directly, none of eleven open-weight models gets more than 4\% of sexagenary days or 1\% of collisions right, and supplying the attributes of every pillar does not change the collisions. A complete list of computed relations is not enough either: it leaves Qwen3-32B at 1\%, with relations listed for other pairs misattributed to the queried pair, whereas a query-filtered layer from follow-up runs, stating only the queried relations pillar by pillar, gives 92\%. On chess positions, two predictions locked in advance hold: attributes do not help, and unqueried relations are again misattributed. Three proprietary reasoning models compute 68--95\% of collisions unaided, while reasoning in smaller open models overrides supplied facts. A fact layer should compute the interactions and return the ones the question asks about.

52. 【2611.13596】owards Reliable VLM Judges: State-Conditional Invariance and Presentation-Aware Diagnostics

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13596

作者：Qinan Zhang,Xinyu Wang,Qihang Jin,Tianyuan Wang,Hongbing Luo,Pengfei Li,Wenjun Lv,Yu Kang,Jian Di

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computer Vision and Pattern Recognition (cs.CV); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Vision-language models, multimodal and embodied, aggregate accuracy, accuracy or consistency, automated judges

备注： Accepted to NeurIPS 2026 (Track on Evaluations and Datasets). 47 pages, 16 figures. Project page: [this https URL](https://xinyuw922-pixel.github.io/Towards-Reliable-VLM-Judge/) ; Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/xinyuw922-pixel/Towards-Reliable-VLM-Judge) ; Data: [this https URL](https://huggingface.co/datasets/nahco318/Towards-Reliable-VLM-Judges)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Vision-language models (VLMs) are increasingly used as automated judges for multimodal and embodied tasks, yet aggregate accuracy or consistency alone does not reveal whether a judge changes its verdict for the right reason. We propose State-Conditional Invariance (SCI), a reliability principle requiring a judge to remain stable under semantics-preserving changes in presentation while responding to causal interventions that change the task outcome. We instantiate SCI in GridWM-Judge, a simulator-grounded diagnostic benchmark built from deterministic MiniGrid trajectories with aligned Full, evidence-removal, and counterfactual variants, plus presentation and representation probes. Across five diagnostic tasks, GridWM-Judge tests local perception, action-conditioned transitions, presentation stability, representation exchangeability, and Full-CF causal discrimination. Our diagnostic evaluations reveal heterogeneous failure modes, including sensitivity to evidence presentation and encoding, low activation on successful trajectories, and weak paired counterfactual discrimination. Perception-controlled analyses further indicate that visual grounding and objective recovery are substantial, model-dependent bottlenecks. These results motivate jointly reporting correctness, presentation stability, and causal sensitivity instead of relying on a single score.

53. 【2611.13595】IFRS Compliance Verification: An Open Multi-LLM Benchmark and Content-Conditioned Ensemble Classifier

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13595

作者：Aghoghomena Akasukpe,Li Yang,Xinyao Zhou,Chen Shen,Miguel Vargas Martin

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：Financial Reporting Standards, International Financial Reporting, Verifying corporate financial, corporate financial disclosures, experts cross-reference disclosures

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Verifying corporate financial disclosures against the International Financial Reporting Standards (IFRS) is a core auditing task in which experts cross-reference disclosures with hundreds of fine-grained regulatory paragraphs: a laborious, costly, and error-prone process that motivates growing interest in large language models (LLMs) as compliance assistants. Yet single-model accuracy remains insufficient for high-stakes use, and naive ensembling is vulnerable to systematic per-model biases that can drag aggregate accuracy below that of the strongest individual annotator. This gap is addressed with a multi-stage framework, supported by a new benchmark of 19,998 IFRS regulation-disclosure pairs comprising 11,161 unanimous multi-LLM labels and 8,837 human annotations on items where the LLMs disagreed. The framework couples a three-stage domain-adapted sentence embedding pipeline (BERT masked language modeling on SEC EDGAR filings, unsupervised TSDAE and SimCSE adaptation, and supervised contrastive alignment) with a learned ensemble classifier that fuses frozen embeddings and LLM probability features from GPT-4.1, Claude Sonnet 4, and Gemini 2.5 Pro via Tree-structured Parzen Estimator (TPE) optimized gradient-boosted trees. On the disagreement set, the ensemble achieves 81.7% weighted F1, exceeding the strongest single LLM by 10.4 points and majority voting by 14.6 points, and outperforms eleven unsupervised aggregation baselines; the retrieval pipeline attains Recall@15 of 45.3% on unseen regulations. The dataset, model checkpoints, annotation platform, and classifier implementation will be publicly released.

54. 【2611.13578】POEM: Output-Relative Block-Sparse Attention for Long-Context Prefilling

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13578

作者：Haoyi Wu,Yunkai Dang,Wanyu Zhang,Lei Wang,Wenbin Li

类目：Cryptography and Security (cs.CR); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Large language models, shown strong capabilities, Large language, reliable deployment, shown strong

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities across diverse applications, making both safety and utility essential for reliable deployment. Jailbreak attacks use diverse forms of adversarial prompting to bypass alignment safeguards and induce LLMs to generate harmful responses. Some representation-based defenses regulate internal activations using predefined steering directions or learned safety regions. However, adapting intervention to each input without a predefined representational target while limiting over-refusal remains a challenge. In this paper, we propose Target-free Entropy-guided Residual Modulation (TERM), an inference-time defense that uses prompt-calibrated attention entropy deviations to guide adaptive modulation of the model's own residual updates. Attention patterns vary across prompts and decoding steps, making raw entropy values difficult to interpret as a consistent signal for intervention. We therefore calibrate attention entropy against each prompt's own prefill baseline to establish an input-specific reference for reducing unnecessary intervention on benign requests. Adaptive scale normalization and temporal accumulation then account for differences in deviation scale and persistence to determine intervention strength. The resulting signal controls bounded scaling of the model's own residual updates, without relying on a predefined safety direction. We compare our method with seven defenses on seven jailbreak attacks and two over-refusal benchmarks across Llama, Qwen, and Gemma models, including Qwen3 models from 0.6B to 32B. Results show our method improves jailbreak robustness while maintaining low over-refusal on benign safety-sensitive requests with modest inference overhead.

56. 【2611.13568】Pair-Merge Tokenisation Is Hard to Approximate on Binary Strings and Strongly NP-Hard on Unary Datasets

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13568

作者：Kundan Baliga,Aiden Xie,Aditya Byju

类目：Data Structures and Algorithms (cs.DS); Computational Complexity (cs.CC); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Byte-Pair Encoding, shorten text, fewer tokens, text by repeatedly, repeatedly merging

备注： 34 pages, 11 figures. Code and certificates: [this https URL](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24023730)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Tokenisers such as Byte-Pair Encoding (BPE) shorten text by repeatedly merging an adjacent pair of symbols into a new one; fewer tokens mean less computation. We ask whether an efficient algorithm can choose $k$ merges that leave a string of length $n$ almost as short as possible; doing nothing is already within a factor $n$. We prove that, unless P = NP, no polynomial-time algorithm comes within a factor $c\,n/\log n$, for some constant $c0$, already for one binary string and for BPE-style merges that replace every occurrence. The key idea is that $k$ merges form a grammar with $k$ rules: a vertex-cover gap becomes a grammar-size gap, which we amplify with relabelled copies, encode in binary and realise with explicit merges. For merges that may skip occurrences, a simple algorithm achieves $O(n/\log\log n)$, and no Karp reduction can prove hardness beyond order $n/\log n$ unless coNP $\subseteq$ NP/poly. Over a one-letter alphabet, both tokenisation problems left open by Kastreva et al. are strongly NP-complete on datasets with repeated strings.

57. 【2611.13564】A Concept-Based Interpretable Framework for Clinical Text-based Prediction via Token-Concept Attention without Concept Supervision

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13564

作者：Jifan Gao,Junjie Hu,John Caskey,Madeline Oguss,Simona Cristea,Anoop Mayampurath,Matthew Churpek,Majid Afshar,Guanhua Chen

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：Concept-based interpretation, concept, promising approach, approach for explaining, Objective

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Objective: Concept-based interpretation has emerged as a promising approach for explaining blackbox models using human-understandable, high-level concepts. However, most existing methods require annotated concept labels to train the model to identify concepts in the input, and obtaining such annotations in clinical text is often expensive. This study aims to enable concept-based interpretation for language models without concept supervision on clinical prediction tasks. Methods: We propose a novel framework that enables concept-based interpretation without concept supervision through a tailored cross-attention bottleneck. Clinical text and the text descriptions of medical concepts are first tokenized and embedded by a pretrained language model. A cross-attention module then estimates the presence of each concept for each text token, producing concept relevance scores that capture how concepts are involved throughout the note. These scores are pooled and used in a linear prediction layer to model clinical outcomes in an interpretable manner. Results: The performance of the proposed framework was compared against blackbox models across two tasks: chest trauma severity stratification using a private dataset and an in-hospital mortality prediction task using the publicly available MIMIC-III dataset. The proposed framework achieved AUROC values within 0.02 of the corresponding blackbox models. The framework also enabled cohort- and patient-level interpretation, capturing the overall concept importance across the cohort and patient-specific concept contributions. Conclusion: Our proposed framework realizes concept-based interpretation without requiring ground-truth concept presence labels. It improves the transparency of clinical text predictions while maintaining strong performance in clinical prediction tasks.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Benchmark scores for medical large language models (LLMs) are difficult to interpret when scoring criteria cannot be traced to clinical evidence. Here we developed Med Evidence Bench (MEB; this https URL), comprising 1,536 physician-constructed items across three levels: guideline knowledge, standardized clinical scenarios and real-world laboratory scenarios. Each criterion stating a medical claim is linked to a versioned source-text span.
