These findings from Chinese-language materials indicate that guideline recall does not ensure patient-specific evidence use and do not establish clinical effectiveness.

59. 【2611.13560】Are Dense Rewards Worth Their Tokens in GRPO?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13560

作者：Vikash Singh,Weicong Chen,Debargha Ganguly,Yanyan Zhang,Mohsen Hariri,Sreehari Sankar,Wang Yang,Chaoda Song,Alexander Nemecek,Osama Zafar,Yuting Shao,Kai Ye,Biyao Zhang,Xinpeng Li,Shouren Wang,Erman Ayday,Jing Ma,Sanmukh Rao Kuppannagari,Vipin Chaudhary

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：RLVR trains language, correct final answers, rewarding correct final, RLVR trains, trains language models

备注： 59 pages (10 pages of main text), 18 figures, 27 tables. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/vicky157/grpo-validation)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:RLVR trains language models to reason by rewarding correct final answers. A widely used algorithm, group-relative policy optimization (GRPO), gives every token of a solution the same credit, so dense process rewards have been proposed to credit intermediate steps, for example with a Monte-Carlo reward that scores each step by sampling continuations from it. Comparing such a reward with GRPO per training update, or vetting it with a gradient test on a single batch, leaves out what it costs: in our setting, the continuations alone take 91% of all generated tokens. We introduce a matched-compute protocol that gives every training intervention the same budget, measured in generated tokens and training GPU-hours, and tests it in preregistered studies with controls for side effects. Across 1,668 runs on six models, matching compute reverses the ranking. On Qwen2.5-1.5B, the Monte-Carlo reward leads standard GRPO per update, yet ends 14.3 percentage points lower at equal tokens and 7.8 points lower at equal training time, and none of four dense-reward variants outperforms GRPO even with as much training time. A per-token gradient test orders these rewards as training does but overestimates each by one to two points, as much as the gains it is meant to detect. In contrast, training on the prompts whose predicted success is nearest 1/2 improves accuracy at matched compute, by 1.7 points on Qwen3-0.6B and 0.8 on Qwen2.5-1.5B. These gains replicate on fresh seeds and unseen problems, and a simple rule, that the candidate prompts are predicted to fail more often than not, predicted where they appear in five of six preregistered tests. Both gains come early in training, and standard GRPO with twice the initial learning rate comes within half a point of them. We argue that training interventions should be validated at matched compute, which overturns some apparent gains and confirms others.

60. 【2611.13557】Darmok: Championship Cryptic Crossword Solving via Constraint Tightening and Resilient Orchestration

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13557

作者：Siddhartha Chatterjee(The University of Texas at Austin)

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing (cs.DC)

关键词：British cryptic crosswords, British cryptic, reach superhuman performance, Unlike American-style crosswords, Times Crossword Championship

备注： 60 pages, 10 figures, 15 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We present Darmok, the first automated system to reach superhuman performance on British cryptic crosswords. Unlike American-style crosswords, cryptic clues disguise an unmarked definition and compositional wordplay beneath a misleading surface reading, sparse grids leave nearly half of each word's letters unchecked, and tournament rules rank a grid with even a single wrong or blank square behind all error-free solutions regardless of speed. Competing alongside an elite human field at the 55th Times Crossword Championship in London, Darmok solved all seven championship puzzles error-free and finished faster than every human competitor in every round. It solved the Final puzzle in 6m 52s, a full 3m 13s ahead of the human champion. Today's frontier AI models can on average solve a whole puzzle in a single try with comparable accuracy and speed. However, cloud API calls still stall, time out, trigger safety refusals, or get stuck in reasoning loops. To simultaneously achieve tournament-grade accuracy, speed, and reliability, Darmok cross-checks clues across three independent models in rounds; uses verified crossing letters to unlock harder clues; and uses a whole-puzzle model and dictionary lookups to fill any remaining gaps without overwriting confirmed words. This design maintained near-zero-error performance across a five-month daily evaluation period. Under normal benchmark conditions, removing backup models, fallback steps, or elaborate prompt instructions barely changes accuracy, making the architecture appear over-engineered. Yet during both the worst days of daily operation and a severe slowdown of our primary model in the live Championship Final, the backup paths carried the solve, showing that as foundation models master the average case, the central challenge in building complex AI systems shifts to resilience in the worst case.

61. 【2611.13552】How Post-Training Influences Verbalized Algorithmic Computation in Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13552

作者：Niall Dalton

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Post-training improves language-model, remains unclear, chain of thought, improves language-model reasoning, improves language-model

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Post-training improves language-model reasoning, but how it changes the use of internal information and verbalized chain of thought (CoT) remains unclear. We study successive SFT, DPO, and RLVR checkpoints of Olmo-3-7B-Instruct on multi-digit addition, using carry values as known intermediate state. Probes recover carry information from internal activations with similar accuracy in SFT and RLVR, although manipulating this information does not reliably control model outputs. In contrast, the usefulness of verbalized reasoning differs: CoTs produced by RLVR yield higher accuracy across receiving checkpoints than those produced by SFT or DPO. We also show that carry-related statements receive higher importance scores after RLVR than matched random controls. DPO shows similar sensitivity to carry-related statements but without comparable accuracy gains. These results suggest that post-training changes the usefulness of written reasoning and sensitivity to its content in meaningful ways, although greater sensitivity does not necessarily coincide with better problem performance.

62. 【2611.13543】Sanity Checks for Attention Surgery: Null Models and Matched Interventions for Representation-Motivated Design

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13543

作者：Basavaraj R M,Aryan Bharat Kumar

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Transformer architectural modifications, architectural modifications frequently, modifications frequently measure, Transformer architectural, remove it architecturally

备注： 13 pages, 7 figures, 4 tables. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/Basavarajearlybird/SANITY-CHECKS-FOR-ATTENTION-SURGERY_Original) (archived at [this https URL](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.24096410) )

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Transformer architectural modifications frequently measure a statistic on internal representations, observe that it is large, remove it architecturally, and report an improvement. Interpreting that sequence requires evaluating whether the statistic exceeds its noise floor, whether it survives a geometric null, and whether the training gain requires the specific direction removed. We formulate these requirements as three reusable checks and apply them to self-attention removal, which deletes the component of an attention head's output aligned with its own value vector. Across nine multi-head pretrained models (5,408 heads, 124M to 6.9B parameters), an earlier-position causal null accounts for 48% to 63% of the head-value cosine alignment, with no monotone scale trend. Under grouped-query attention, this alignment is specific to each head's own key-value group and reverses across groups (+0.24 to +0.27 within-group, -0.11 to -0.19 across-group). Split-half reliability demonstrates that the per-head intervention effect is resolvable in GPT-2 ($r_\Delta = 0.80$) but attenuated in Pythia-160M (0.42) and Pythia-410M (0.53), whereas structural self-cosine reliability is near-perfect in all three ($r_{\text{stat}} 0.99$); which statistic predicts its own intervention is model-dependent. In a pre-registered 3-arm by 8-seed paired factorial at $4.0 \times 10^8$ tokens per run (399,900,672 tokens), a fixed arbitrary rank-one direction does not differ from baseline (+0.0011 nats, $p = 0.197$), while self-directed removal lowers validation loss (-0.0029 nats, Holm-corrected $p = 0.0023$) and outperforms the fixed arbitrary control directly (-0.0040 nats, $p = 0.0018$). At 50.9M scale the training benefit requires the own-value direction rather than a fixed arbitrary one, but a large cosine alignment alone does not establish redundancy in frozen models.

63. 【2611.13531】Framing-Induced Vulnerability in Large Language Models: Evidence from Chinese News Headlines

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13531

作者：Mo Zhou,Yiwei Zhang

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Large language models, models increasingly evaluate, Large language, language models increasingly, increasingly evaluate

备注： 7 pages, 5 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large language models increasingly evaluate, route, and moderate news, so a model's judgment of a headline has become a gatekeeping decision. We ask whether that judgment follows the fact being reported or the way the fact is framed. We combine an ecological audit of 63,926 Chinese news headlines with a controlled experiment. The experiment covers 30 real events, 30 synthetic events, and 29 natural headline pairs, tested on four Chinese models. Changing only the headline frame shifted both perceived misleadingness and event severity, and the direction and ordering of the shifts were the same for DeepSeek, MiMo, Dots, and LongCat. Emotionally intensified headlines raised severity ratings by 0.65 to 0.88 points, whereas positively reframed headlines lowered them by 0.34 to 0.52 points. Supplying the article body reduced this severity bias by 54 to 80 percent, and all four reductions were significant at p .01. A small human reference study showed the same direction of effect. Headline-only LLM evaluation therefore carries a measurable robustness gap, and framing sensitivity belongs among the reliability properties that deployment and governance check.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Accurate inertial odometry is critical for autonomous systems but suffers from unbounded drift caused by the integration of sensor noise and biases. In approaches such as TLIO, neural pseudo-measurements are trained independently of their downstream filter. 67. 【2611.13498】Evaluating Compositional Generalization in Transformers: The Role of Composition Equivalence and Module Coverage

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13498

作者：Purva Pruthi,Andrew Yuan,Alexander D'Amour,David Jensen

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：unseen combinations, important form, Compositional, module coverage, compositional equivalences

备注： 40th Conference on Neural Information Processing Systems (NeurIPS 2026). Track on Evaluations and Datasets

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Compositional generalization is an important form of out-of-distribution generalization, defined as the ability to train on some combinations of modules (e.g., functions such as sort, filter) and then generalize to unseen combinations. Despite substantial empirical work evaluating compositional generalization in transformers, findings have been inconsistent because evaluations were often conducted on a limited set of train-test splits, defined in an ad hoc manner. Through a systematic evaluation, we identify two key aspects of the train-test distribution that strongly affect performance: composition equivalence and module coverage. We show that the apparent performance of direct models (trained only on final outputs) results primarily from exploiting composition equivalences, meaning different sequences of modules that reduce to identical end-to-end functions. When benchmarks eliminate these equivalences, performance drops significantly, showing their inability to generalize to compositions of known modules that produce novel end-to-end functions. We further demonstrate that performance degrades monotonically as module coverage shifts increase (when modules appear in different contexts between train and test), affecting both direct and step-by-step models (trained on intermediate and final outputs). These findings have concrete implications for benchmark design: benchmarks that include compositional equivalences can lead to significant overestimation of the generalization abilities of direct models, and benchmarks that include both module coverage violations and compositional equivalences can exacerbate shortcut learning. As a case study, we demonstrate the role of compositional equivalence on the generalization performance of the pre-trained model Llama3-8B on the code-output prediction task in the coding benchmark LiveCodeBench.

71. 【2611.13414】Free Lunch Uncertainty: Sequence-Level Rényi Entropy from Particle Decoding for Adaptive LLM Reasoning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13414

作者：Erfan Baghaei Potraghloo,Milad Heidari Sorkhehdizaj,Shahab Rahimirad,Aliasghar Pourghani,Seyedarmin Azizi,Souvik Kundu,Abolfazl Hashemi,Joseph Campbell,Vijay Gupta

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Quantifying uncertainty over an entire LLM completion is critical for selective prediction and adaptive compute. Zero-cost signals such as mean token entropy and log-probability are local to tokens or to one completion, and self-consistency needs many extra completions. We show that Sequential Monte Carlo (SMC) decoding for power sampling already estimates the normalizing constant of the power distribution, which gives the sequence-level R'enyi entropy (SRE) of the model's completion distribution at zero marginal cost, with a guarantee at every particle budget. A R'enyi chain rule extends this readout to a family of stepwise entropies of any order. We prove that orders above one are tied to the most likely token of each step, which explains why they track log-probability, while the order-1/2 member accumulates the log of per-step search effort. This member is the best zero-cost selective-prediction signal in 10 of 13 settings. On MATH500 its AUROC is 0.022 to 0.044 above log-probability with all four models and within length quartiles, and the same order is selected when each setting is held out. An 8-particle probe identifies the problems that benefit from more particles, and a two-pass policy matches 64-particle accuracy on MATH500 with 34.2% fewer particles over three seeds.

74. 【2611.13339】Measuring Definition Sensitivity in LLM Annotation of Social Science Concepts

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13339

作者：Bogdan Mamaev,Qizhou Wang,Christopher Leckie,Matteo Vergani

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Computers and Society (cs.CY)

关键词：social science research, manual process, science research, traditionally a costly, social science

备注： Accepted at the NeurIPS 2026 Workshop TAE: Can We Trust AI Evaluation? Robustness, Causality, and Risk in Modern AI Assessment

点击查看摘要

Abstract:In social science research, text annotation is a fundamental task that is traditionally a costly, hard-to-scale manual process. Researchers have used large language models (LLMs) to perform annotation by writing a definition of a concept into a prompt - a rule that determines whether a label should be attributed to a text depending on its content. However, concepts such as hate speech, fake news, and terrorism typically have several theoretically defensible competing definitions. It is also unknown whether the model actually applied the definition to provide the label or the label was based on the model's pretrained notion of the concept. To test this, we have the model annotate the same texts twice, each time with a different definition. We call the disagreement between the two sets of annotations definition sensitivity. As a control, we paraphrase one definition but leave its requirements unchanged - the disagreement between the annotations with the two wordings becomes the paraphrase floor. Across six concepts and three LLMs, a swap of competing definitions always changes more annotations, on average, than a paraphrase. This shows that the annotations depend on the prompted definition, so any set of annotations should be interpreted together with the definition that produced it. The LLM annotator's definition sensitivity and its paraphrase floor should both be reported in such work.

75. 【2611.13322】MicroLens: Read, Trace, and Rewire the Residual Stream with Two Lenses

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13322

作者：Nika Zahedi,Fangyi Zhu,Anna Wu

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Human-Computer Interaction (cs.HC)

关键词：decode hidden states, inside language models, decode hidden, inside language, logit lens

备注： 9 pages, 11 figures. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/Ni2002ka/residual_stream_vis/tree/v1.0-pacificvis)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Lenses that decode hidden states into words are a popular way to look inside language models, but a heatmap of decoded words only shows what a vector reads as and cannot distinguish whether, or how much, the model uses the information. We present an interactive viewer that puts reading, tracing and editing on the same layers-by-tokens grid of the residual stream. In the viewer, every cell can be decoded with two lenses, the logit lens and the Jacobian lens (J-lens), traced back to the tokens it drew from, and edited, with the rest of the model rerun on the spot. We demonstrate three usage scenarios on Gemma-3-4B-it, backed by sweeps over a few hundred prompts, to show what this tool can discover. First, the two lenses give different directions to write with, each suiting a different edit: the logit lens's word direction sets what a token outputs, while the J-lens's direction changes what later tokens read. Second, in two-hop trivia questions (the capital of the state or country containing a given city), the state or country is readable at the city token from as early as layer 7, and swapping it there can redirect the answer 92-98% of the time; however, at the answer position, the state or country information is not used. Third, when a chat model answers questions with a step-by-step reasoning prompt, the correct answer is often already the top word in the residual stream before any reasoning is written: for 89-92% of retrieval questions and 39-63% of multi-hop and serial ones, far above their respective distractors (at most 5%). However, ordering puzzles are an exception, as the model works them out only once it writes its final reasoning steps. Furthermore, for answers that are visible in the residual stream, the J-lens reads them a layer or two earlier than the logit lens across the different types of question.

76. 【2611.13293】Decode Token Parallelism: Memory-Efficient Distributed Attention Parallelism for Scalable LLM Decoding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13293

作者：Susav Shrestha,Brad Settlemyer,Narasimha Reddy

类目：Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing (cs.DC); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Serving large language, Serving large, large language models, requires KV-cache capacity, large language

备注： 14 pages, 12 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Serving large language models at high concurrency with long contexts requires KV-cache capacity and attention bandwidth that grow with the decode pool. Existing parallelization strategies trade one against the other: data parallelism replicates model weights, limiting KV capacity; tensor parallelism stops scaling attention at the model's KV-head count and pushes dense collectives across nodes; and decode context parallelism (DCP) recovers capacity by sharding the sequence but adds cross-node aggregation of partial attention results. We observe that during decode, dense layers and attention scale differently: dense layers amortize weight reads across the batch and run efficiently within a single NVLink domain, whereas attention cost grows with every active request's KV cache. We present Decode Token Parallelism (TKNP), a decode-pool architecture that decouples weight placement from attention ownership. TKNP distributes a single model copy across nodes in balanced layer blocks. The active node holding a block executes its projections and MLP for the full batch using intra-node tensor parallelism, while every node computes attention for its own requests against locally resident KV cache. This extends request-local attention across nodes without replicating weights: only current-token activations cross the network, dense all-reduces remain within NVLink, and attention requires no cross-node merging of partial results. Implemented in vLLM and evaluated on Llama 3.1 405B and Nemotron-4 340B on up to 64 H100 GPUs, TKNP improves completion throughput for long-context, high-concurrency workloads by up to 1.7$\times$ over DCP and by up to 2.06$\times$ over data parallelism at matched GPU count.

77. 【2611.13270】What drives the emergence of symbolic mechanisms in large language models?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13270

作者：Melody Zixuan Li,Taylor Webb

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Recent work, support abstract reasoning, large language models, symbolic mechanisms, work has identified

备注： 25 pages, 15 figures, 5 appendices. To appear at NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent work has identified a set of emergent symbolic mechanisms that support abstract reasoning in large language models, but it remains unclear what factors drive the emergence of these mechanisms. To address this question, we trained neural networks from scratch on abstract sequence tasks, varying both architectural and data distributional factors, and investigated the effects of these factors on the emergence of symbolic mechanisms. Using representational, attentional, and causal mediation analyses, we first confirmed that transformer-based language models trained from scratch on our task developed symbolic mechanisms. We then found that, across multiple axes of data diversity, greater diversity produced stronger symbolic signatures. Surprisingly, the same signatures emerged in modified transformer architectures and even multilayer perceptrons, indicating that architectural inductive biases are not necessary for the development of symbolic mechanisms. We also observed consistent patterns in an entity-attribute binding task. Together, these findings identify data diversity, not architecture, as the primary driver of emergent symbolic computation in neural networks.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13249

作者：Lin Yao

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Multi-teacher on-policy distillation, on-policy distillation assigns, Multi-teacher on-policy, typically updates shared, Domain-Routed Delta Optimization

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multi-teacher on-policy distillation assigns each sample a domain teacher but typically updates shared student parameters. We introduce Domain-Routed Delta Optimization (DRD-MOPD), which specifies which parameter components each domain's samples update. The student is the sum of a frozen base and trainable components initialized from expert-minus-base weights. All components participate in every forward pass; four update modes define the correspondence between sample domains and components, each with independent optimizer states. We analyze these update rules and measure retention of the original expert geometry through angles, projection coefficients, and energy. Prior work such as Open-MOPD distills directly from the base model; 97.02% of its released final checkpoint's increment energy lies outside the span of the original expert directions. In comparison, our Own checkpoint after 20 updates retains coefficients near one along each component's original direction and pairwise angles ranging from 88.04 to 92.41 degrees; its exported BF16 student's increment has 4.90% outside energy, versus 10.52% for the sum of saved FP32 components. The same checkpoint achieves a 32.72% macro score across six benchmarks, compared with 32.44% for its initial task-arithmetic merge. These measurements describe geometry and capability at the evaluated training stages and settings.

79. 【2611.13223】Deletion Sensitivity Is a Property of the Model: A Three-Condition Measurement across Seven Open Reasoning Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13223

作者：Hanzhang Jia,Liheng Zeng,Zhiyu Yang,Yi Gao,Bo Ma

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Software Engineering (cs.SE)

关键词：Reasoning models write, multi-turn tool session, turns read, Reasoning models, tool session

备注： 10 pages, 5 figures, 8 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Reasoning models write a reasoning trace at every turn of a multi-turn tool session, and later turns read this history. Across 94k ShareGPT conversations, 73 percent of user interactions span multiple turns. Upstream providers bind each trace to its issuer, so a request forwarded across issuers arrives with its history deleted, and the task cost of this stripping remains unmeasured. To the best of our knowledge, this paper presents the first direct, blind, multi-seed measurement of full reasoning-history deletion in multi-turn tool calling, through three rendering conditions, nine open-weight reasoning models, three seeds, 794 tasks, and the official checker. The same deletion spans -3.9 to +51.3 points with both ends excluding zero, and for the one model re-dosed from 0 to 100 percent deletion, the reading varies by at most 1.5 points. Deletion sensitivity is a model-level property. The reading follows the model itself and stays constant across deployments, and a retrained or re-released model is a new model that a two-cell screen, one retention run and one deletion run, re-reads for the cost of two runs. Deletion sensitivity is thus a measurable property of the model, and the reading must enter routing and aggregation as prior information rather than persist as an invisible default cost.

作者：Bo Li,Chulian Zhang,Or Biri,Anthony Chang,Anton Korzh,Noam Duvdevani,XingFei Xi,Daniel Stokes,Zhenhuan Chen,Ludwig Schneider,Artem Y. Polyakov,Julien Demouth,Chandler Zhou

类目：Distributed, Parallel, and Cluster Computing (cs.DC); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Performance (cs.PF)

关键词：Expert parallelism, shards the experts, small batch sizes, GPUs, Expert

备注： 19 pages, 8 figures, 14 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Expert parallelism (EP) shards the experts of a Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) layer across GPUs, so every MoE layer requires two all-to-all communications, namely dispatch and combine. Compared with training, inference processes relatively few tokens per GPU at a time, and at small batch sizes fixed costs that do not grow with the number of tokens, rather than data movement, dominate the latency. We present NVLinkOneSided, a one-sided EP all-to-all for GPUs within a single NVLink domain. Each source rank pushes its tokens, without first exchanging metadata, into rank-major buffers on the target GPUs, and the grouped GEMMs read them in place instead of from a per-expert copy. We provide two protocols for the synchronization that the communication requires, namely Fence and Sentinel, for high-throughput and low-latency scenarios respectively. On Blackwell GPUs, NVLinkOneSided completes dispatch and combine 1.2-3.0$\times$ faster than DeepEP, with the largest speedups at small batch sizes. The resulting MoE layer is up to 2.4$\times$ faster than DeepEP with DeepGEMM. It even outperforms the fused DeepGEMM MegaMoE at up to 128 tokens per GPU, making it still competitive for decoding. We expose NVLinkOneSided through a modular interface, so that it is agnostic to the activation quantization format, decoupled from the MoE compute backend and easy to integrate into inference frameworks.

83. 【2611.13140】Agreeing on the Answer but Not the Reason: Measurement Limits of LLM Reasoning Failure Taxonomies

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13140

作者：Madhu Sudhan Rao Beesetty

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：model errors fall, taxonomies for LLM, LLM reasoning, papers state, named categories

备注： 11 pages. Accepted at IEEE CogMI 2026 (regular paper)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Failure taxonomies for LLM reasoning are increasingly reported as measurements: papers state what fraction of a model's errors fall into named categories, then compare those fractions across models. We show that for one such category the reported fraction is largely a property of the measurement procedure. We defined Compositional Collapse (a model notices a required sub-part of a problem, then drops or misapplies it) and measured it across twelve models and eleven languages on a public multilingual math benchmark (2,316 graded records). First, 50.1% of failures carry evidence for more than one category, so the reported plurality depends on the order in which the classifier tests them: reversing two tests moves it in eleven of twelve models, and across all six orderings Collapse's share spans 26.4% to 76.4%. Second, agreement collapses for the category but not for the task. Native speakers of all eleven languages judged 179 transcripts, agreeing with our inspector at kappa = 0.932 on answer correctness but only kappa = 0.093 on Collapse. A second native speaker per language re-rated 142 items; on 121 determinate pairs the two humans agree with each other at kappa = 0.659, while each agrees with the inspector at kappa = 0.094. Re-running the same inspector model with the human instructions verbatim raises kappa against Human 2 from 0.147 to 0.636, so the gap is largely attributable to the prompt, not the model. Third, the inspector labels Collapse 1.81 times as often as native speakers, but only 1.17 times once a defective benchmark item (a deleted clause in the Bengali and Chinese translations) is excluded. We recommend four cheap checks before comparing failure-mode rates across models: report label co-occurrence, permute the tie-break, validate on a shared independent sample, and audit systematic disagreements.

84. 【2611.13135】Which Successes Make Good References? Action-Sequence Alignment for Multi-Turn On-Policy Distillation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13135

作者：Mufei Li,Nikki Lijing Kuang,Zhuoer Wang,Samson Koelle,Jie Yuan,Manqing Mao,Hong Wang,Yanjun Lin,James Feng,Daniel Edmiston,Pan Li,Wei Niu

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：train-inference distribution mismatch, On-policy distillation, reduces train-inference distribution, train-inference distribution, distribution mismatch

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:On-policy distillation (OPD) reduces train-inference distribution mismatch by supervising a student on its own trajectories. For multi-turn tool-use agents, conditioning the teacher on a validated same-task successful trajectory as a reference can provide useful hindsight, but different successes may reach incompatible interaction states and offer misleading supervision. We cast successful-reference selection as a free-target optimal transport problem, which reduces to assigning each failed trajectory its most compatible successful reference. Because latent state paths are hidden, we use action-sequence alignment as an observable proxy for compatibility and instantiate it with longest common subsequence (LCS), which identifies a shared ordered backbone of actions while allowing recoverable detours. This perspective makes trajectory compatibility the central criterion for reference selection in multi-turn OPD. Experiments on ALFWorld and the airline domain of $\tau^3$-bench show that LCS is the only evaluated reference selector to improve over reference-free OPD on both ALFWorld overall scores and all three airline metrics. Without further optimization, the airline-trained models also transfer to the retail domain, where LCS improves all three metrics.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Retrieval-augmented language model systems generally select context by query-conditioned relevance. We argue that long-lived AI systems require a second operator: activation-conditioned memory availability. We specify an architecture in which request and goal cues seed an authorized memory graph, activation and fan attenuation allocate a bounded traversal frontier, retrieved entities recursively emit cues, and a typed evidence graph is reconstructed before generation. The specification yields five architectural consequences about candidate selection, traversal control, graph regularization, evidence reconstruction, and reinforcement. We derive implementation-level cost estimates and define ablations, failure conditions, and a reproducible evaluation protocol. The central claim is narrowed or falsified if a simpler graph reranker matches full traversal on causal and temporal recovery under equal budgets. This architecture-and-analysis preprint reports no benchmark measurements. If the claim holds, retrieval returns the evidence path that makes a memory available, not only a ranked list.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13114

作者：Erik Derner,Jiří Kubalík,Robert Babuška

类目：Robotics (cs.RO); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG); Systems and Control (eess.SY)

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:System identification requires expert decisions on the identification method, incorporation of prior physical knowledge, and model validation. We propose ASPIRE, a framework for nonlinear system identification in which a large language model agent selects and executes the identification method. ASPIRE constructs closed-form, nonlinear static or dynamic models from measured data, optionally supplemented by a system description, the intended use of the model, and physical requirements expressed formally or in natural language. 87. 【2611.13107】Catch It Where It Happens: Real Time Error Correction During LLM Reasoning

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13107

作者：Rotem Elimelech,Michael Shalyt,Ido Kaminer

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Large language models, Large language, trusted with mathematical, scientific and engineering, engineering work

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly trusted with mathematical, scientific and engineering work, yet their mistakes are often confidently written and hard to spot. A single wrong step tends to propagate, making the model waste inference tokens following a false premise. Existing safeguards either check only the final answer and restart from scratch, or rely on a verifier LLM that competes for GPU capacity and slows down response time. We propose online, deterministic verification: many kinds of reasoning steps can be validated by code, so a step can be verified in real time, as it is written, without any model call or GPU use. Whenever a check fails, we point out the mistake and let the model continue from the same spot. We demonstrate this approach on symbolic mathematics, where derivations already contain explicit expressions that a computer algebra system can check in milliseconds. Tested on the ASyMoB benchmark with three open-weights models, our online validation improves accuracy by 1.4-5.6 percentage points, corresponding to a 1.8-14.9% net reduction in incorrect answers, while increasing token usage by only 6-7%. Because the verifier is independent of the model, the approach complements progress in LLMs rather than competing with it: every new model can benefit immediately, and the benefit grows as deterministic checkers extend to more kinds of reasoning steps.

88. 【2611.13077】One Temperature to Rule Them All?

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13077

作者：Aviv Orly(1),Ori Shem-Ur(1),Yaron Oz(1) ((1) School of Physics and Astronomy, Tel Aviv University, Israel)

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：improving large language, large language models, Inference-time sampling, improving large, large language

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026. 82 pages, 45 figures, 6 tables. Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/avivorly/one-temperature)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Inference-time sampling is a central mechanism for improving large language models, with temperature controlling the tradeoff between reliability and diversity. While recent work suggests that varying temperature across samples can improve multi-sample inference, simple fixed-temperature schemes remain the standard in practice. We investigate this discrepancy in the canonical pass@K setting, asking when temperature diversity is useful, and when a single tuned temperature suffices. To this end, we analyze per-question success probabilities across multiple models and datasets, tracking how they vary with sampling temperature. We identify several consistent properties of these profiles, including that harder questions are less likely to share efficient sampling temperatures, although this trend is only logarithmic in hardness. We then derive an efficient method for evaluating the theoretically optimal multi-temperature allocation scheme, and show that although such a scheme improves performance, its gains over single-temperature baselines are typically modest when temperature is tuned as a function of the sampling budget K. We explain this phenomenon through mathematical mechanisms that we call dulling effects, and show how they ensure the robustness of temperature choices across different question distributions. Our results clarify when multi-temperature sampling is useful, and how temperatures should ideally be chosen.

89. 【2611.13061】he Left Ripple of Byte-Pair Encoding: Certified Lookbehind for Every Dictionary

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13061

作者：Nicklas Nidhögg

类目：Formal Languages and Automata Theory (cs.FL); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Data Structures and Algorithms (cs.DS); Programming Languages (cs.PL)

关键词：formalized byte-pair encoding, Merwe formalized byte-pair, der Merwe formalized, van der Merwe, appended string

备注： 87 pages, 13 tables. Artifact: [this https URL](https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.23988871) . The programs and their outputs are in the artifact, with a verifier that reruns every program and holds each reported number to the output line it rests on. Companion to [arXiv:2608.03473](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.03473) , [arXiv:2608.09761](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.09761) , [arXiv:2609.10600](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10600) , [arXiv:2610.08854](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.08854) and [arXiv:2610.30168](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.30168)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Berglund and van der Merwe formalized byte-pair encoding and bounded how far an appended string can change a tokenization, leaving the converse open: how far can a string prepended on the left change it, which dictionaries make that ripple infinite, and can it be computed efficiently when finite? We answer all three for every dictionary. The origin radius, how far a prepend can change covering spans, is decided with radius certificates by a product of two runs of the automaton that guesses the final tokens, in time polynomial in the dictionary written out, and it is infinite exactly when one periodic word has two canonical tilings with no common boundary. Under their ordering condition it is infinite exactly when some rule (t, t) finds its two copies adjacent at its own stage, and such a rule makes any dictionary infinite. A finite radius makes every window of a width read off the radii have a cut, and the radius is finite exactly when such cuts are dense. The token machine completes for every dictionary, and the tokenization is subsequential, extending what Berglund, Martens and van der Merwe proved for proper ones under one pass per rule. A streaming tokenizer emits after every symbol exactly the tokens that no continuation changes, in polynomial time, answering a question of Dalirrooyfard and Ye. Boundedness of the label measure, the edit distance of the token sequences, is decided for every dictionary in polynomial time as well, and under the ordering condition the largest distance one edit causes over dictionaries of m rules lies asymptotically between m^2/10 and m^2/2. GPT-2's merge table carries 244 effective self-pairs, so its origin radius is infinite from the forty-second merge on, and that of cl100k, o200k and Llama 3 from their first. Every measured number is its program's output, and what is not executed is named.

点击查看摘要

These are compression results only: we have not yet evaluated a model trained with v4 or v5.

94. 【2611.13012】khammoon: From a Linguistic Guideline to a Thai Morpheme Segmenter

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13012

作者：Warit Sirichotedumrong,Adisai Na-Thalang,Chanakan Wittayasakpan,Kritsadha Phatcharoen,Kunat Pipatanakul

备注： Technical Report. Code, models, and data are available at [this https URL](https://github.com/warit-s/khammoon-segmenter)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Thai is written without spaces between words, making segmentation a foundational step in most Thai NLP pipelines. Existing tools primarily reproduce the word-level conventions of corpora such as BEST; however, because the notion of a "word" in Thai lacks a unified linguistic consensus, these conventions struggle with productive compounding, leading to arbitrary boundaries and ambiguity in downstream representations. Instead, we propose operating at the morphological level, focusing on khammoon, the smallest atomic unit that carries meaning. Segmenting at the morphemic level provides an explicit, consistent morphological representation that eliminates compounding ambiguity. Crucially, this representation benefits downstream learning: models trained on morphemes perform on par with or exceed word-level baselines across part-of-speech tagging, named-entity recognition, and sentiment classification, demonstrating that linguistic consistency improves representation quality without downstream performance penalty. To operationalize this representation, we translate linguistic principles into a formal decision guideline with near-perfect human agreement ($\kappa \approx 0.99$), scale annotation with an LLM, and correct systematic errors with human labels. Finally, we train khammoon, a lightweight 1M-parameter convolutional BiLSTM segmenter designed for efficient CPU inference, achieving a boundary F1 of 0.992 on out-of-domain text. The guideline, datasets, model, and library are all publicly released.

95. 【2611.13006】Seeing Is Not Believing: Counterfactual Lesion Erasure Teaches a Small Medical Vision-Language Agent to Report Only What the Image Shows

作者：Bhaskar Gurram

备注： 28 pages, 1 figure, 13 tables. Preprint v1.0; reader study in progress. Code, data recipes, pre-registrations and per-view outputs: [this https URL](https://github.com/bhaskargurram-ai/medscope)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Medical vision-language models (VLMs) often report a finding because the question names it; standard benchmarks miss this. The evidence sensitivity gap (ESG) is the rate of absent-or-refer answers after erasure minus the rate after the control edit; it is zero for a model that ignores the image or reacts to both edits alike. MedScope-4B (Qwen3.5-4B, LoRA, image tools) was trained on label-derived trajectories, selected by a pre-registered rule and tested once against nine frontier and six open VLMs; four pre-registered checks then probed the mechanism. On 507 internal cases across five imaging types, MedScope-4B reached ESG 0.758 and claimed an erased lesion in 9.6% of cases, against 0.27-0.47 and 41-62% for the frontier models (all p0.001), without significant loss of accuracy on real lesions. Without counterfactual views ESG fell to 0.279+-0.107 (three seeds); the effect replicated on MedGemma 1.5 4B and two unseen inpainting tools. At four external centres the advantage held but frontier models detected more real lesions; on a fresh external test MedScope-4B detected 0 of 48 pneumothorax films, with net utility below gemini-4-argon's (-0.36 [-0.50, -0.23]). Matching the control edit was not shown to matter; over three seeds no fresh-external ESG advantage over removal-based DPO was established; a paired-view consistency objective did not beat a matched-compute control, while that continuation alone cut erased-lesion claims on unseen tools at every seed, at a cost in sensitivity at one. Training on erased and edited images gives a small model an evidence-gated disposition that prompting does not give frontier models; it does not improve perception, and rare critical findings need coverage.

96. 【2611.12998】Linear Probes to Bayesian Manifolds: Geometry of Arithmetic in Large Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12998

作者：Anshul Kumar,Neha Prasad,Manoj Kumar,Barnabás Póczos,Deeksha Varshney

类目：Machine Learning (cs.LG); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：study geometric structures, large language models, Linear probes, geometric structures, helices and circles

备注： 26 pages, 4 figures, 20 tables. Under review at ICLR 2027

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Linear probes are a standard method to study geometric structures, such as helices and circles, from the activations of large language models (LLMs), and such structure is often taken as evidence that the model computes with it. Probe accuracy alone cannot establish this, because it conflates four separate claims. These are (1) that the concept is decodable from the activations, (2) that they form a specific shape, (3) that the shape belongs to the probed concept rather than to a related concept the model also represents, and (4) that the output of the model depends on the shape. We call these claims decodability, shape, ownership, and causal use, and we test them separately with a four-stage Bayesian pipeline. Stage 2 compares candidate shapes and selects the best one only when its evidence passes a predefined threshold. Stage 3 removes a set of related concepts, specified in advance, and tests whether the shape is owned or inherited. Stage 4 intervenes on the shape to test whether the output of the model depends on it. We apply the pipeline to two-digit addition and multiplication in GPT-J 6B, Llama 3.1 8B, and Pythia 6.9B. Nearly every concept is decodable, but the decoded directions overlap substantially across concepts. Every strongly supported comparison selects a ribbon, which is a helix whose radius changes with the value, or a torus. Of 1,330 cells with an ownership verdict, 19% of shapes are owned and 81% are inherited. Owned and inherited shapes move the output about equally often. Intervening on the shape retains approximately 61% of the effect of intervening on the full probe while using at most five dimensions, against a median of nine for the probe. Our results indicate that a probe verdict alone overstates what a model computes with, and we propose that probe results be reported as verdicts on all four claims rather than as a single accuracy.

97. 【2611.12995】Capacity Allocation at the Source: Sparse Target Optimization for LLM Knowledge Editing

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12995

作者：Dongyao Chen,Jiayun Lei,Zhiyuan Ji,Zhiying Deng,Wei Liu,Xiaobo Sun

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：efficiently updating embedded, large language models, updating embedded knowledge, target hidden state, hidden state

备注： neurips2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Knowledge editing has emerged as a promising approach for efficiently updating embedded knowledge in large language models (LLMs). It first computes an ideal target hidden state that steers the LLM toward the new output, and then edits the model parameters so that the original hidden state is mapped to this target. While most existing methods focus on the second stage of parameter editing in preserving other knowledge, we find that the upstream construction of the target hidden state also plays an important role in allocating the model's limited capacity space. We first establish a theoretical connection between interference in the parameter space and interference between target hidden states, showing that the two are positively correlated. Building on this insight, we propose a simple yet effective $\ell_1$ regularization method that prunes away unnecessary ``tentacles'' of each knowledge vector, retaining only the essential components. With fewer extraneous tentacles, each edit affects less other knowledge, enabling better coexistence. Extensive experiments across multiple LLMs and benchmark datasets verify the effectiveness of our method. Code: this https URL.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12992

作者：Javier Marín

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Energy-based models, assign a scalar, predicted variables, option, observed and predicted

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Energy-based models (EBMs) assign a scalar energy to every joint value of the observed and predicted variables. In decision models, the prediction is chosen from a finite list of options provided with each input. Inference then reduces to comparing the energies of the options, and training lowers the energy of the correct option relative to the others. A complete decision must also account for the possibility that no option is acceptable. However, the energies learned by a standard EBM define only relative compatibility: adding the same input-dependent quantity to all energies leaves the predictions, the Gibbs distribution, and the usual training losses unchanged. The model therefore has no learned reference level against which to decide whether any option fits. We propose ZeroPoint, an EBM that makes the empty answer a state of the model with energy zero for every input. This fixed state provides an explicit reference for the energies of all listed options, whose non-negative depths measure their compatibility with the input relative to the empty answer. The options and the empty answer share one distribution, in which an option at zero depth receives no probability. The decision to select an option or to abstain is thus part of ordinary inference, trained with the negative log-likelihood loss, rather than a separate rejection rule. The energy function is a small multilingual encoder that reads the input together with all the options and is invariant to their order. Ablations show that most of the benefit comes from training with examples in which no option fits. With the same encoder and data, the fixed zero separates inputs with and without an acceptable option slightly better than a learned rejection class and clearly better than a thresholded softmax, while leaving the choice among options unchanged.

99. 【2611.12937】racing Psychometric Inference in Large Language Models

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12937

作者：Chih-Hao Hsu,Feng-Chun B. Chou,Pin-Hao A. Chen

类目：Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：represent psychological constructs, large language models, language models represent, resemble psychometric structure, large language

备注： Accepted at NeurIPS 2026

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Do large language models represent psychological constructs internally, or do they only generate outputs that resemble psychometric structure? We address this question using a cross-persona paradigm across 14 model variants from two families (Llama 3 and Qwen 2.5, 0.5B-14B, base and instruct). Given responses on one psychological scale from real individuals (N = 272 Taiwanese university students), models predict responses on six other scales. At the behavioral level, larger variants reproduce and amplify human cross-scale correlations; amplification persists after correcting the seven-scale human correlations for measurement attenuation. We then examine whether this structure is reflected internally. Contrastive direction analysis finds geometry aligned with the human matrix in larger instruct variants, but little alignment in base variants under this assay. Across models, geometry strength is associated with behavioral amplification (r = 0.68, p = 0.008; partial r = 0.69 controlling for log size). To relate internal structure to output behavior, a matched activation-probing paradigm shows that probe slopes vary less than behavioral slopes (1.38-1.77 vs 0.52-1.53). A synthetic control shows that ridge probing can inflate these slopes; after subtracting a baseline based on a reliability proxy from another analysis, adjusted slopes remain associated with behavior (r = 0.88). We term the measured probe-behavior gap readout attenuation. These results establish decodability and geometric alignment under the tested assays, without showing that the measured directions mediate the cross-persona answers.

100. 【2611.12922】Using LLM Agents to Evaluate Prosocial Interventions on Social Media Platforms

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12922

作者：Lucen Li,Erin Wertz,Muhammad Haroon,Anshuman Chhabra,Magdalena Wojcieszak

类目：ocial and Information Networks (cs.SI); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Social media platforms, Social media, Social, media platforms, Abstract

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Social media platforms are a primary source of information for many users. Although research explores various interventions to promote prosocial content or demote harmful content on platforms, there are few systematic methods for evaluating competing intervention designs before conducting costly human-subject experiments. We develop a simulation-based evaluation pipeline built on the OASIS framework. We generate 13,835 LLM-based agents, each with a unique profile. Over 20 time steps, we expose them to real-world Twitter/X posts and let them comment, like, repost, or follow. We use those agents to systematically evaluate three interventions that increase exposure to verified news: adding content, upranking content, and celebrity source cues. All interventions significantly increase news engagement, with upranking news to prominent feed positions producing the largest effects. These effects hold across agents' gender, age, and education levels. We validate our injection treatment results against a comparable human-subject study and find strong directional alignment and similar dose-normalized efficiency. Our results suggest that LLM-based agent simulations can support early-stage evaluation of platform interventions before human-subject deployment, and upranking may be more effective than news volume or source.

101. 【2611.12918】IntentTwin: Deconfounding Candidate Support and the Oracle-to-Deployment Gap in Offline Web Behavior Prediction

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12918

作者：Miaobo Hu,Shuhao Hu,Xiaobo Guo,Xin Wang,Bokun Wang,Daren Zha

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Information Retrieval (cs.IR); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：web-behavior benchmarks frequently, benchmarks frequently insert, Offline web-behavior benchmarks, measure reranking, finite candidate set

备注： 26 pages, 10 figures

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Offline web-behavior benchmarks frequently insert the demonstrated action into a finite candidate set and then measure reranking. We show that this protocol can create a candidate-support confound: when the target is outside training support while all distractors are sampled from that support, support membership identifies the gold action without modeling behavior. We formalize this effect with support-identifiability rate (SIR), construct support-balanced candidate sets, and evaluate a target-free dual-path proposer that combines support retrieval with open-vocabulary DOM/LM proposals. On filtered OPeRA (4,282/469/992 train/dev/test steps), the original protocol has SIR 1.0000 on target-OOV rows and a support-only rule reaches 1.0000 OOV Exact@1. Support balancing reduces SIR to 0.0000; the strongest conditional reranker obtains 0.7214 overall Exact@1, 0.7988 on IV rows, and 0.5668 on OOV rows. The target-free dual-path system reaches Proposal Recall@16 0.6915 and end-to-end Exact@1 0.5212, compared with oracle conditional Exact@1 0.7450. On Mind2Web, the same audit yields SIR 0.2380 and an oracle-to-proposal gap of 0.2125. A 200-row semantic audit labels 0.7150 of exact-key OOV targets as semantically novel, 0.2450 as locator/string variation, and 0.0400 as ambiguous (Cohen's $\kappa=0.8173$). These measurements show why support composition must be controlled alongside candidate accuracy when offline results are used to reason about deployment.

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12913

作者：Jiahao Qin,Yiwen Wang

类目：Multimedia (cs.MM); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

备注： 21 pages, 5 figures, 6 tables

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Multimodal humor detection requires interpreting language, vocal delivery, and visual behavior jointly. Expressive fusion must also regulate how interactions of different orders enter the final judgment. We propose Möbius Interaction Filtering (MoIF), which learns a modality-subset prediction function and imposes order-selective spectral regularization through its aggregation structure. Private temporal encoders and balanced reciprocal interaction construct supervised unimodal, bimodal, and trimodal predictors. CMU-MOSI and CMU-MOSEI evaluations establish applicability to sentiment prediction, and cross-seed analyses show more stable estimates on all three datasets. MoIF connects the organization of multimodal interactions to their statistical reliability through an executable spectral constraint. Code is available at this https URL.

104. 【2611.12904】Actionable Feedback: Improving Model Capabilities from User Edit Data on Clinical Documentation

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12904

作者：Daniel P. Jeong,Yewon Byun,John Giorgi,Peng Xu,Zachary C. Lipton,Michael Oberst

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI); Machine Learning (cs.LG)

关键词：Large language models, Large language, clinical documentation workflows, clinical documentation product, clinical documentation

备注： Preprint

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly being deployed in clinical documentation workflows, where clinicians review and edit their outputs before use. In principle, these clinician edits represent a scalable source of supervision for model training. In practice, not all edits represent actionable feedback: for instance, on data drawn from real-world deployment of an AI clinical documentation product, our review suggests that over 45% of edited outputs contain user-added information that is not directly supported by the inputs to the model. Naively fine-tuning on such edits risks teaching the model to hallucinate. In this paper, we develop a method to address this challenge. Given that real clinician edits on deployed documentation tools are not publicly available, we first develop a taxonomy of clinician-editing behaviors, informed by data from a deployed AI clinical documentation product. Then, we develop a method for generating semi-synthetic clinician-edit data, informed by our taxonomy, on a discharge-summary generation task based on MIMIC-III, a public dataset. Finally, we introduce simple filtering methods for identifying actionable edits, with accuracy evaluated by expert review. We show empirically, on our semi-synthetic dataset, that naive fine-tuning on raw edited output increases ungrounded content, while our approach of progressively filtering toward grounded edits enables improvement on clinician-alignment metrics while retaining faithfulness to the input context.

105. 【2611.12903】SELD: Detecting Silent Evidence Loss in Context Compression beyond the Answer

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12903

作者：Wentao Lei,Li Liu

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：silent evidence loss, Evidence Loss Detector, evidence loss, long inputs, silent evidence

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Context compression is now routine for language models with long inputs, but a compressed context is useful only if it keeps the evidence for the answer. Existing checks compare the reader model's answer or its most likely words, and thus miss a frequent failure, silent evidence loss: key evidence is removed while the answer stays the same, leaving the answer unsupported and risky for other models that reuse the context. To address this challenge, we propose SELD (Silent Evidence Loss Detector), which needs no training and no labels. Its principle is that when evidence is lost, a reader model's preferences among the answers it leaves unsaid shift even if its answer does not, so comparing these preferences before and after compression reveals losses that answer checks cannot see. We also build SEL-Pairs, a benchmark of length-matched evidence and control deletions from three question-answering datasets. Quantitative and qualitative experiments with five reader models on it show that SELD consistently outperforms strong and recent baselines across compressors, compression rates and models, identifying the evidence-removing edit in 83% of matched pairs with unchanged answers, against 70% for the strongest baseline. These results show that evidence loss in compressed contexts can be detected even when the answer is unchanged.

106. 【2611.12902】meOmni-o1: Bidirectionally Coupling Time Series Foundation Models and LLMs for Multimodal Reasoning and Forecasting

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12902

作者：Ziyang Zhang,Shenyi Li,Wen Wu,Chao Zhang

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：Time series, series, Time, TSFM, LLM

备注： Code: [this https URL](https://github.com/OpenTSLab/TimeOmni-o1) Project page: [this https URL](https://windowso.github.io/TimeOmni-o1-web/)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Time series from real-world systems are often accompanied by text, such as a report on an event that affects them. Two common tasks on these series are understanding, e.g., answering questions about a series, and forecasting its future values. Both tasks require reasoning over the series and the text together. Moreover, understanding outputs text while forecasting outputs a time series, so serving both requires a model that reads and generates both modalities. Large language models (LLMs) read time series as digit tokens and handle long series poorly. Time series foundation models (TSFMs) forecast well from large-scale pretraining but typically neither read nor generate text. Time series language models add a time series modality to the LLM but forecast on the LLM side, leaving the TSFM's pretrained forecasting ability unused. We present TimeOmni-o1, which couples a pretrained TSFM and an LLM bidirectionally. In one direction, the TSFM encodes the series in compressed form for the LLM, which reasons over it together with the text. In the other, the LLM's reasoning is fed back into the TSFM, which produces the forecast conditioned on it. One model architecture thus reads and generates both series and text, reasons in text, and draws on the TSFM's pretrained forecasting ability. To train it, we build MMTR, a multimodal time series reasoning dataset with annotated reasoning traces, covering both tasks across 12 domains with 500k+ samples. On MMTR, TimeOmni-o1 outperforms existing models on both understanding and forecasting across most domains, as well as the LLM and the TSFM inside it when used individually. The gains carry over to three open benchmarks, where it outperforms the dedicated time series models.

107. 【2611.12901】Rethinking in Spikes: Mitigating Hallucinations in MDLMs with Step-Aware Decoding

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.12901

作者：Zhongxing Xu,Zhonghua Wang,Zhe Qian,Shiyan Su,Ming Hu,Xiaocheng Zou,Wei Feng,Mingquan Lin,Yuyin Zhou,Yifan Peng,Hamid Rezatofighi,Zongyuan Ge

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Artificial Intelligence (cs.AI)

关键词：exhibited strong global, strong global modeling, global modeling capabilities, Recent advancements, diffusion language models

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Recent advancements in multimodal diffusion language models (MDLMs) have exhibited strong global modeling capabilities through iterative refinement. We observe that mask uncertainty follows an overall downward trend during iterative denoising, while a few denoising steps exhibit localized entropy spikes closely associated with hallucinations. We argue that anomalous steps can be identified by measuring the entropy deviation of each denoising step from its neighboring temporal window. We hypothesize that discrete token commitment discards the distributional information at masked positions, limiting the continued interaction between candidate semantics and the context during entropy spikes. With this goal, we present StepRefine, a plug-and-play decoding strategy that leverages semantic context to achieve reliable denoising. The core of our method lies in a step-aware switch between discrete denoising and latent refinement. The model employs probability-weighted embeddings at entropy spike steps to preserve diverse reasoning hypotheses and performs multiple rounds of continuous refinement. Meanwhile, a refinement controller monitors distributional stability to reduce unnecessary loops and switch the model back to standard discrete denoising. Through extensive experiments, StepRefine demonstrates significant hallucination mitigation across different MDLMs on multimodal benchmarks.

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Longitudinal social-media histories offer an opportunity to assess depression, potentially supporting the inference of standardized depression questionnaire responses. Existing approaches rely on semantic similarity to retrieve posts, which can introduce misleading or non-attributable evidence, particularly in multi-symptom posts and discussions of others' experiences. We propose SREE (Symptom Retrieval and Evidence-Grounded InfErence), an evidence-grounded pipeline that extracts first-person and symptom-specific text spans, selects a representative subset using a Facility Location-inspired objective, and performs Evidence-Gated Inference over the selected evidence. We evaluate evidence retrieval on BDI-Sen and evidence-conditioned questionnaire inference on eRisk 2019-2021. Compared with state-of-the-art retrieval approaches, SREE achieves relative improvements of 70% in post-level precision and 59% in F1, while retaining at least 30% fewer posts. These results highlight the importance of localizing attributable evidence and controlling its composition before inferring depression questionnaire responses.

109. 【2611.14039】hermodynamics of words

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14039

作者：Klaus M. Frahm,Dima L. Shepelyansky

类目：atistical Mechanics (cond-mat.stat-mech); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Physics and Society (physics.soc-ph)

关键词：word thermalization hypothesis, American National Corpus, words, thermalization, total

备注： 30 pages, 6 main figures, 14 appendix figures, journal style file and bbl file for references provided

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We introduce the word thermalization hypothesis according to which in a literary work there are effective interactions between words that drive them to thermalization where their word frequency distribution is the same as for the phenomenon of Rayleigh-Jeans (RJ) thermalization and condensation. In this concept, the different words play a role of classical oscillators (particles) and their word frequencies, or related tokens, are analogous to certain filling factors over energy state levels. The thermalization process is characterized by the conservation of total system energy, or total number of words/tokens, and total probability norm, or total number of different words. We apply this approach to analyze 16 literary works, including Bible, complete works of Shakespeare, and data from American National Corpus. We show that for these cases the effective system energy is very low and thus there is the RJ condensation phenomenon with a big fraction of total probability concentrated at the lowest energy states corresponding to a big fraction of rare words appearing only once or twice in the whole text. Such a RJ condensation is observed for light propagation in multimode optical fibers and other physical systems. We relate the thermalization of words to the recently proposed wealth thermalization for households in world countries. The economy concepts of Lorenz and Pareto curves, and also Zipf curves, are applied to the word frequency distributions of the selected 16 cases and it is shown that the thermalization theory gives a strikingly good description of real data.

110. 【2611.13689】Walrus Technical Report

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13689

作者：Alkis Koudounas,Osamu Take,Hayato Futami,Quentin Jodelet,Emiru Tsunoo

类目：Audio and Speech Processing (eess.AS); Computation and Language (cs.CL)

关键词：handling speech recognition, foundation model handling, emotion recognition, general audio understanding, present Walrus

备注： 30 pages, 3 figures, technical report

点击查看摘要

Abstract:We present Walrus, a unified 8B speech-and-audio foundation model handling speech recognition (including code-switching and multi-talker speech), speech-to-text and speech-to-speech translation, text-to-speech, emotion recognition, audio captioning, and general audio understanding. Our main contribution is not a new architecture but a data-centric training recipe; we use a standard decoder-only backbone initialized from Gemma 4, so that gains can be traced to the data. For speech generation, Walrus predicts low-bitrate discrete speech tokens, and a lightweight token-to-waveform decoder converts them into high-fidelity audio. The recipe is a three-stage data curriculum that moves from broad multilingual data to multi-talker, code-switching, and domain-specific data. At each stage, task-aware and dataset-aware weighting balances tasks with very different token budgets and protects core skills from forgetting. Trained on over 600M samples covering more than 20 tasks and 100 languages, Walrus leads on most multilingual, multi-talker, and code-switching ASR, speech translation, and audio understanding benchmarks, outperforming both open speech language models of comparable size and dedicated task-specific models. On most benchmarks, it even matches or surpasses the $4\times$ larger Qwen3-Omni's performance. Results suggest that data engineering is a primary driver of quality in multi-task speech and audio modeling.

112. 【2611.13400】Orthographic depth at two scales, with English also matched for lexicon size

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.13400

作者：Adam Y. Shavit

类目：Applications (stat.AP); Computation and Language (cs.CL); Neurons and Cognition (q-bio.NC); Methodology (stat.ME)

关键词：predictably spelling maps, English, maps to sound, struggle to read, proposed reason

备注： 38 pages, 5 figures. Spelling-to-sound entropy as a measure of orthographic depth for cross-language reading research; exploratory check against Grade 1 reading accuracy in Section 3.5. Code and data: [this https URL](https://doi.org/10.17605/OSF.IO/A9T3C)

点击查看摘要

Abstract:A reader can struggle to read in only one of their languages, and one proposed reason is how predictably spelling maps to sound. We measure that predictability as conditional entropy, the pronunciation uncertainty left once the spelling is seen, at two grain sizes (unit sizes): the alignment unit, an aligned spelling-sound chunk, and the syllable, bounded in the pronunciation. We analyse word lists in twelve alphabetic orthographies and report five in full, with English pronunciations from a dictionary and the rest from the synthesizer eSpeak-ng, length marks removed. Among the five, English has the most uncertainty and Finnish the least at both grains. Across all twelve, Portuguese and Danish exceed English per alignment unit, and German and Italian order differently by grain and transcription. These orders hold when English is transcribed by eSpeak-ng with u allowed to spell /j/ plus a vowel. Uncertainty per unit has no shared direction as units grow: it falls in Spanish and, less securely, German, is level in Finnish, depends on transcription in Italian and on the word list in English, and rises in Norwegian and Swedish. Summed uncertainty per word falls in all twelve, but that score is handed the pronunciation's boundaries and mixes context with in-sample memorisation, so it is no evidence of what a reader computes. An exact identity states when discarded spelling boundaries could reverse that fall. A uniform 20,000-word English sample holds 35% of the dictionary's syllable spellings and has syllable entropy 0.098 bits lower, so lexicons must be matched in size. In an exploratory check over eleven languages, syllable-scale entropy tracked first-year reading accuracy (rho = -0.74, falling to -0.25 if English is given the lowest entropy instead), without separating the syllable scale from the alignment unit. These estimates describe sampled writing systems, not readers.

2. 【2611.14325】Point-in-Time Encoders for Earnings Calls

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Text encoders turn earnings calls into vectors for search, for language models that answer from retrieved passages, and for forecasting models. A live retriever can use an encoder trained once on all available text, as finance retrievers are, but a backtest needs an encoder that read no text written after the date of each vector. To address this look-ahead bias, from 412,248 calls we train vintages under four pretraining recipes, with yearly cutoffs from 2010 to 2024, and each vintage reads only text dated on or before its cutoff. We then fine-tune each vintage on the question-answer pairs of the calls themselves. The vintages we train on calls alone, with 33.8M parameters, retrieve answers on held-out calls better than the general retrievers we tested in every test quarter. Their vectors also raise by about half the rank correlation between next-day returns and forecasts built from past returns and firm size. When each vintage continues the previous one, a later vintage retrieves better on earlier quarters than the vintage available at the time, and a point-in-time language model that answers from the passages the later vintage retrieves inherits the gain. The 2024 vintage of our families also forecasts earnings surprises better, although the interval of this gain includes zero on calls that no vintage read. It forecasts returns no better, so point-in-time vintages matter most for retrieval and little for forecasts of returns.

3. 【2611.14236】StaRSE: Compact Russian Sentence Embeddings with a Sign-Coded Static Encoder

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14236

作者：Boris Malashenko,Ivan Jarsky,Egor Soldatov,Vladislav Luzhnov,Sviatoslav Stumpf,Vladislav Shmatkov,Viacheslav Shalamov,Andrey Stankevich,Valeria Efimova

类目：Computation and Language (cs.CL); Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

备注： Accepted to ReNeuIR @ SIGIR 2026. 16 pages

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Transformer sentence encoders provide strong retrieval quality, but their inference cost becomes a bottleneck when embeddings must be produced on CPUs or refreshed frequently. We introduce StaRSE, a compact Russian static sentence encoder that removes Transformer inference at deployment time: token directions are stored as sign bits, token norms are stored separately, and sentences are encoded by mean pooling over norm-scaled sign directions. StaRSE is initialized from ruBERT-base, fine-tuned on Russian adjacent-sentence and task-shaped pairs, and post-trained with a contrastive stage that directly optimizes the sign-projected token table. On RuMTEB, StaRSE reaches a task mean of $51.16$, $7.98$ points above the strongest included static baseline. The packaged model occupies $11.3$ MiB and has a mean CPU latency of $0.08$ ms per sentence, compared with an estimated $55.7$ MiB 16-bit parameter footprint and $1.43$ ms mean CPU latency for the closest-quality contextual encoder. Ablations examine adjacent-sentence supervision, token norms, and direct sign-projected post-training.

4. 【2611.14220】SHARP: Semantic Hierarchical Agentic Reasoning for Next POI Prediction with Dynamic Memory

链接：https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.14220

作者：Yuhang Zhan,Silin Zhou,Zhuo Chen,Haoxiang Lei,Yunren Feng,Lisi Chen,Shuo Shang

类目：Information Retrieval (cs.IR)

关键词：supports personalized location, personalized location services, POI prediction, prediction supports personalized, POI

备注：

点击查看摘要

Abstract:Next point-of-interest (POI) prediction supports personalized location services by anticipating users' next destinations. Recent methods leverage large language models (LLMs) for semantic reasoning and graphs for collective mobility modeling, improving POI prediction. However, three challenges still remain. Bridging LLMs' semantic reasoning and specific POI prediction remains challenging. User mobility also evolves over time, making it necessary to retain and update historical knowledge within limited LLM contexts. Meanwhile, global transition graphs smooth out time-specific category transition patterns. To address these challenges, we propose Semantic Hierarchical Agentic Reasoning for Next POI Prediction with Dynamic Memory (SHARP), an agentic hierarchical framework in which category-level reasoning guides specific POI prediction. A trajectory memory agent maintains a dynamic trajectory memory bank that preserves earlier mobility patterns and tracks behavioral changes with limited context growth. We construct multi-granularity time-aware category transition graphs with global and time-conditioned views to preserve temporal regularities obscured by global aggregation. A category predict agent combines these graphs with trajectory memory to predict likely next categories. SHARP learns semantic IDs (SIDs) through a residual-quantized variational autoencoder (RQ-VAE) to construct semantic POI representations, which are jointly optimized with a category-guided POI predictor end-to-end. On three real-world datasets, SHARP improves Acc@5 and Acc@10 by an average of 5.29 and 4.52 percentage points, respectively, over the strongest baseline.

Abstract:As multimodal tourism-related information collected from heterogeneous data sources has become increasingly available, learning effective representations has attracted attention for improving recommendation performance. Multimodal information collected from heterogeneous data sources is first semantically unified into a shared textual representation space using large language models (LLMs).
